docs/research/hyperscaler-best-practices-2026-05-12.md:**SRE practice: error budgets + 4 golden signals.** SLO drives release decisions: if the error budget is healthy → ship; if depleted → freeze and stabilize. The four golden signals — latency, traffic, errors, saturation — are the canonical dashboard. Burn-rate alerts (1× = on-target, 3× = exhaust budget in 1/3 of window) are now standard. ([Google SRE — Monitoring Distributed Systems](https://sre.google/sre-book/monitoring-distributed-systems/); [Google SRE Workbook — Error Budget Policy](https://sre.google/workbook/error-budget-policy/); [Splunk — Four Golden Signals](https://www.splunk.com/en_us/blog/learn/sre-metrics-four-golden-signals-of-monitoring.html))

docs/decisions/ADR-0345-oss-stewardship-class-policy-and-cve-response-sla.md:  - feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20
docs/decisions/ADR-0345-oss-stewardship-class-policy-and-cve-response-sla.md:This ADR resolves the collision by introducing **stewardship class** as a deliberately non-tier terminology. The three classes — Maintainer / Contributor / Consumer — are relationship labels, not numeric levels. The `oya-governance-stewardship-class-vocabulary` lane (§E.7) refuses any drift toward the word "tier" in stewardship contexts. Per `feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20` discipline, the corpus has already retired one prior tier ladder (Bronze / Silver / Gold / Platinum capability tiers); reusing "tier" here would re-introduce exactly the kind of ambiguity that discipline retired.
docs/decisions/ADR-0345-oss-stewardship-class-policy-and-cve-response-sla.md:- `feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20` — the corpus has retired one prior tier ladder; the stewardship-class terminology is non-tier deliberately.
docs/decisions/ADR-0342-api-versioning-hybrid-date-public-semver-sdk.md:- ADR-0316 (tier system canonical Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum) — RETIRED; informational; version is orthogonal to capability-tier and tenant-class.
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:  - feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:- **ADR-0329** Tier system retirement — retires Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum and the surrounding tier vocabulary.
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:Per ADR-0329, the Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum tier ladder is retired. Replacement is not "drop the tier names and keep going." Many µservices encoded tier semantics into:
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:**Versioning + deprecation.** IP-tenant-class-adoption.md is versioned per ADR-0108. Sunset of an adoption surface (e.g., if billing_components changes) follows ADR-0138 six-path deprecation. ADR-0329 retirement (Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum) happens in parallel; ADR-0331 adoption fills the resulting gap.
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:Per ADR-0329, Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum names retire from the corpus. The retirement happens in the same edit as the tenant_class adoption — a µservice does not retire its tier names then later add tenant_class; both surfaces are touched in one IP per D-13.
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:- ADR-0329 Tier system retirement — retires Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum, the predecessor model.
docs/decisions/ADR-0331-cross-microservice-tenant-class-adoption-template.md:- feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20 — paired tier retirement directive.

docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:  CHECK (capability_tier IN ('observer','contributor','supervised_operator','compensated_worker','clinical_trainee','research_trainee','portfolio_only'))
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:F.2 Map each existing field to `program_type`, `capability_tier`, `program_tenant_id`, and `program_membership_status`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.0 Primitive `capability_tier` is part of the ADR-0320 conformance surface.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.1 Source of truth: `capability_tier` is defined by `oya-shared-program-transient-identity` or by a table generated from that crate contract.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.2 Tenant rule: `capability_tier` must be evaluated against personal, source, host, and program tenant context when those tenants are present.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.3 ADR-0244 rule: `capability_tier` cannot introduce a new tenancy primitive outside the universal tenant model.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.4 ADR-0311 rule: `capability_tier` cannot merge personal and work identities into one host-owned record.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.5 ADR-0313 rule: `capability_tier` must preserve child-tenant sovereignty when the host belongs to a conglomerate.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.6 ADR-0292 rule: `capability_tier` must block unsafe minor flows when age, guardian, or school facts are missing.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.7 ADR-0299 rule: `capability_tier` must be available for fairness audit when used in evaluation, ranking, pay, or conversion.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.8 ADR-0305 rule: `capability_tier` must expose mentor or supervisor context when capability depends on supervision.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.13 Failure mode: if `capability_tier` is missing, services may treat a participant as a generic employee, generic student, or generic guest.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.14 Test fixture: include active, expired, revoked, suspended, minor, cross-border, and portfolio-only cases for `capability_tier`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:I.9.15 Audit fixture: include creation, mutation, denial, and revocation events for `capability_tier`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:M.5.1 Test `activation_denies_unknown_capability_tier` is required for ADR-0320 implementation acceptance.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:M.5.2 Test `activation_denies_unknown_capability_tier` must include the tenant tuple and policy version in its fixture.
docs/decisions/ADR-0320-apprentice-intern-resident-fellow-transient-identity.md:M.5.3 Test `activation_denies_unknown_capability_tier` must run against the shared crate contract before any consumer service claims conformance.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: C (composed across crm + workflow-engine + ontology + intelligence + marketplace). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Sales Cloud Essentials/Professional → Oyatie **Bronze** CRM; Enterprise → **Silver**; Unlimited + Einstein 1 Sales → **Gold**; Sales Cloud + CPQ + Revenue Cloud → **Platinum** (with marketplace+contract-lifecycle-management composition).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- **Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316**: Sales Cloud Professional → Oyatie **Bronze** CRM (basic Account/Contact/Opportunity, manual forecasting); Sales Cloud Enterprise → **Silver** CRM (Territory Management, Approvals, Process Builder/Flow equivalents); Sales Cloud Unlimited + Einstein 1 Sales → **Gold** CRM (Einstein Lead/Opp Scoring + Einstein Forecasting + Conversation Insights composed with `intelligence`); Sales Cloud + Revenue Cloud + CPQ → **Platinum** (with `marketplace` DealSet + `contract-lifecycle-management` composition for quote-to-cash + subscription billing).

docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Gold** on `data-pipeline` (bidirectional-sync + CDC + warehouse-publish); **Silver** on `workflow-engine` (programmable action only — not full DAG); **Silver** on `intelligence` (data-quality recommendations only); **Bronze** on `compliance` (basic audit; full data-class tagging is Snowflake/Catalog responsibility).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `itsm` (full ticket lifecycle, SLA, multi-brand, multi-channel ingest); **Gold** on `community` (multi-brand Help Center + community-posts + translation-tree); **Silver** on `messenger` (side-conversations only — full messenger is D-067 Chat); **Silver** on `intelligence` (Answer Bot + macro-suggest with EU-AI-Act provenance); **Bronze** on `crm` (requester customer-360 binding only — full CRM is D-051).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `messenger` (full multi-channel conversation + bot + proactive); **Gold** on `intelligence` (bot-flow + intent + entity NLU + handoff); **Silver** on `community` (chat-widget on portal embed only); **Bronze** on `comms-sms` + `comms-whatsapp` (per-channel rail integration only — full SMS/WhatsApp rail is D-Twilio).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `contact-center` (full voice + IVR + ACD + recording + LNP + supervisor); **Gold** on `intelligence` (transcription + sentiment-analysis with EU-AI-Act provenance); **Silver** on `itsm` (Talk-to-ticket bridge with screen-pop); **Bronze** on `cloud-iac` (per-tenant SIP-trunk + DID provisioning).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Gold** on `messenger` (multi-channel in-app + email + SMS); **Gold** on `intelligence` (Fin AI Agent with KB-grounding + custom-answer); **Gold** on `community` (product-tour + checklist + banner + tooltip + news + help-center); **Silver** on `marketing-automation` (outbound + series — full marketing-automation is HubSpot Marketing Hub D-053 / Marketo); **Bronze** on `crm` (Lead + User + Company tracking only).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-warehouse` (full OLAP with per-tenant workload-isolation + Iceberg-open-format + time-travel + replication); **Gold** on `intelligence` (Cortex-LLM + Cortex-ML + Cortex-Search; embedded inline in SQL); **Gold** on `marketplace` (data-share + native-app per ADR-0249); **Silver** on `data-pipeline` (Snowpipe + Streams + Tasks); **Silver** on `compliance` (tag-based governance + masking-policy + clean-rooms); **Bronze** on `developer-sdk` (Snowpark UDF/procedure + Streamlit-app hosting).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-warehouse` (lakehouse SQL + Photon + Iceberg-UniForm + DBSQL); **Platinum** on `intelligence` (MLflow + Mosaic AI + Vector Search + Agent Eval); **Gold** on `data-pipeline` (DLT + Streaming Tables + Lakeflow Connect); **Gold** on `compliance` (Unity Catalog governance + lineage + tags + audit + cleanrooms); **Silver** on `marketplace` (Delta Sharing + Databricks Marketplace + Databricks Apps per ADR-0249); **Silver** on `developer-sdk` (Databricks SDK + Asset Bundles + Databricks Connect); **Bronze** on `cloud-iac` (cluster provisioning via Terraform-provider).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-warehouse` (serverless + slot-reservation + materialized-views + Iceberg-BigLake + Object Tables + cross-cloud-Omni); **Gold** on `intelligence` (BQML + Gemini-in-BigQuery with EU-AI-Act provenance); **Gold** on `data-pipeline` (Dataform + Datastream CDC + BigQuery Data Transfer Service); **Gold** on `compliance` (Data Catalog + DLP + Policy Tags + Row-Access-Policies + Column-Masking-Policies + Dataplex governance); **Silver** on `marketplace` (Analytics Hub per ADR-0249); **Silver** on `cloud-cdn` (BI Engine in-memory cache); **Bronze** on `developer-sdk` (BigQuery DataFrames + bq CLI + client-libraries).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-warehouse` (provisioned + serverless + Spectrum + materialized-views + concurrency-scaling); **Gold** on `marketplace` (Data Sharing inbound + outbound + cross-region per ADR-0249); **Silver** on `intelligence` (Redshift ML — SageMaker AutoPilot-shape with EU-AI-Act); **Silver** on `data-pipeline` (COPY + Streaming Ingestion + Zero-ETL); **Silver** on `compliance` (RLS + column-level + Lake Formation tag-based access); **Bronze** on `cloud-iac` (cluster/workgroup provisioning via Terraform/CDK).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Gold** on `data-pipeline` (transform-DAG runner with model + test + snapshot + seed + macro + package + exposure); **Gold** on `data-warehouse` (semantic-layer via MetricFlow; metric + semantic-model + entity + dimension + measure); **Silver** on `developer-sdk` (dbt-project git repo + dbt-CLI analog + Jinja templating); **Silver** on `intelligence` (semantic-layer for AI grounding + dbt-Copilot equivalent with EU-AI-Act); **Bronze** on `compliance` (model-contract + source-freshness + test-severity).

docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-pipeline` (event-stream ingest + 400+ destination fanout + tracking-plan governance + Functions transformation); **Gold** on `intelligence` (Personas/Unify identity-resolution + computed-traits + SQL-traits); **Gold** on `marketing-automation` (Engage outbound + journey-orchestration); **Gold** on `compliance` (Privacy Portal + DSAR + consent-categories + IAB TCF); **Silver** on `data-warehouse` (warehouse-destination + reverse-ETL); **Silver** on `crm` (unified profile + identity-graph); **Bronze** on `marketplace` (destination catalog as marketplace per ADR-0249).
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:**Capability-tier mapping (ADR-0316).** **Platinum** on `data-pipeline` (event-stream warehouse-first + 200+ destinations + Cloud Extract + Reverse ETL + transformation-as-code); **Gold** on `developer-sdk` (transformation-as-code in JS/Python in customer git repo; SQL Profiles models in dbt-style); **Gold** on `data-warehouse` (warehouse-first orientation; warehouse is the source-of-truth, not vendor-hosted store); **Silver** on `intelligence` (RudderStack Profiles with identity-graph + traits in dbt-style SQL); **Silver** on `compliance` (Data Catalog + Tracking Plans + DSAR via Profiles); **Silver** on `marketing-automation` (Audiences + activation — less developed than Segment Engage); **Silver** on `crm` (warehouse-resident unified profile).

docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: D (microservice composition: `incident-management` (alert + on-call + escalation + paging anchor with D-084 PagerDuty superset) + `itsm` (Jira / Jira-Service-Management postmortem + ticket bridge) + `community` (Statuspage component bridge) + `comms-sms` + `comms-voice` + `comms-push` + `comms-email` (paging rails) + `workflow-engine` (alert-policies + automation-rules + OEC remote action) + `intelligence` (alert-correlation + noise-reduction + Investigator) + `developer-sdk` (Opsgenie REST API + SDKs) + `apigw` (heartbeat + integration ingestion) + `compliance` (audit + per-tenant data-residency)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Opsgenie Free / Essentials (basic alert routing + on-call schedule + escalation) → **Bronze**; Standard (full alert-policies + automation-rules + heartbeat + service-status + reports) → **Silver**; Enterprise (Incident Investigation + Stakeholder-Comms + advanced ChatOps + cross-team visibility + advanced reporting) → **Gold**; Enterprise + Atlassian-Cloud-Premium / Enterprise data-residency (EU / FedRAMP / Govt) + Customer-Managed-Keys via Atlassian + cross-region failover + audit-event-stream + EU-AI-Act-conformance-for-Investigator + compliance pack (HIPAA / SOC2 / GDPR / KR-PIPA) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability anchor on `siem` (anchor µservice — Splunk pioneered the SIEM pattern) + `observability` (logs + metrics + traces + RUM + synthetic) + `secops` (SOAR + UEBA) + `intelligence` (ML-Toolkit + Anomaly-Detection) + `workflow-engine` (SOAR playbooks + adaptive-response actions) + `data-pipeline` (Splunk forwarders + HEC + Data-Stream-Processor + Edge-Processor) + `developer-sdk` (REST API + Splunk Cloud REST + SDKs)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Splunk Free / Splunk Enterprise indexer-only + basic search → **Bronze**; Splunk Enterprise + Enterprise Security app + ITSI basic + Observability Essentials → **Silver**; Splunk Cloud + Enterprise Security + SOAR + Observability Cloud (APM + IM + RUM + Synthetic + Log-Observer) + UBA + ML-Toolkit → **Gold**; Splunk Cloud + ES + SOAR + Observability Cloud + UBA + Federated-Search + Edge-Processor + GovCloud + Customer-Managed-Keys + cross-region failover + FedRAMP-High + Splunk-AI-Assistant + multi-tenant MSSP → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability anchor on `observability` (logs + metrics + traces + RUM + synthetic + profiler + DBM + NPM anchor — Datadog set the modern unified-observability pattern) + `siem` (Cloud SIEM) + `secops` (CSM + CWS + ASM) + `intelligence` (Watchdog + Bits AI) + `workflow-engine` (Workflows + Service-Level-Objectives + Incidents Auto-Action) + `developer-sdk` (Datadog API + SDKs) + `apigw` (per-source intake-key + per-tag scope) + `data-pipeline` (Observability-Pipelines-Worker for in-transit transform/route) + `compliance` (audit + per-region data-residency)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Infrastructure-only (Pro) → **Bronze**; Infra + APM + Logs (Enterprise) → **Silver**; full unified observability (Infra + APM + Logs + RUM + Synthetic + DBM + NPM + CI/CD/Test Visibility) + Cloud SIEM + ASM + Profiler + Service Catalog + Watchdog → **Gold**; full stack + CSM + CWS + Sensitive Data Scanner + Bits AI + Observability Pipelines + DDR-Mode (data-residency) + Customer-Managed-Keys + FedRAMP-Moderate + Datadog-Government → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability anchor on `observability` (APM + Infra + Logs + Traces + Browser + Mobile + Synthetic + Errors + Profiler + DBM + NPM) + `intelligence` (Lookout + Grok + New Relic AI + Model Performance Monitoring) + `secops` (Vulnerability Management) + `workflow-engine` (Workflows + Destinations + Alerts) + `developer-sdk` (NerdGraph GraphQL API + REST API + SDKs) + `data-pipeline` (Infinite-Tracing + Pipeline-Cloud-Streaming-Service) + `apigw` (License-Key + Insert-Key + User-API-Key) + `compliance` (audit + per-region data-residency US/EU/FedRAMP)). docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `compliance` (eSignature legal-validity + ESIGN + UETA + eIDAS + 21-CFR-Part-11 + HIPAA + FedRAMP + DOD-IL4 — DocuSign is the eSignature + CLM category leader; differentiated by global jurisdiction coverage + CLM + AI contract analytics under one platform) + `workflow-engine` (Maestro + envelope workflows + CLM workflows + Connect webhooks) + `intelligence` (Insight AI + Negotiate AI) + `developer-sdk` (eSignature REST API + CLM REST API + Click API + Connect webhooks + Embedded Signing + SDKs) + `apigw` (per-account + OAuth-JWT + OAuth-Authorization-Code + integration-key) + `cloud-billing` (per-envelope + per-seat + per-add-on billing)). docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `cloud-billing` (invoice + payment + tax + AP automation + Pay anchor) + `marketplace` (supplier catalog + RFx + contracts + SIM + travel + inventory — Coupa is the cloud-BSM category leader; differentiated by community-AI-derived spend benchmarks + sourcing-optimization solver + end-to-end procure-to-pay+contract+expense+treasury under one platform) + `compliance` (Sarbanes-Oxley + ASC-606 + IFRS + e-Invoicing per-country mandates {Italy SdI, France FE, Mexico CFDI, Spain SII, India IRP, Brazil NFe, Saudi-Zatca, etc.} + supplier-diversity + ESG reporting + SOC-1 + SOC-2 + GDPR + ISO + HIPAA-limited) + `intelligence` (Community AI + spend-analysis + supplier-risk-scoring + invoice-OCR) + `workflow-engine` (approval workflows + sourcing event flows + contract workflows + risk-monitoring workflows) + `developer-sdk` (Coupa Open API + cXML + EDI + flat-file integrations) + `apigw` (per-account + OAuth + API-key)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Coupa Express / SMB → **Gold**; Coupa Enterprise (full BSM Suite + Pay + Treasury + Sourcing-Optimization + Community-AI + dedicated CSM + 99.9%-SLA + EU-residency + e-Invoicing-per-country + supplier-diversity + ESG + Customer-Managed-Keys) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `iam` (canonical identity domain — Okta is the workforce-IAM category leader; differentiated by largest pre-integrated app catalog (7,000+ via Okta-Integration-Network OIN) + Universal-Directory schema-flexibility + workflow automation + governance + privileged-access under one identity-fabric) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO 27001 + 27017 + 27018 + 27701 + FedRAMP-High + DOD-IL4 + GDPR + HIPAA-BAA + EU-AI-Act for ThreatInsight) + `workflow-engine` (Okta Workflows — Workato-based no-code automation) + `intelligence` (ThreatInsight + Risk-Based Adaptive MFA + ML-driven anomaly detection) + `developer-sdk` (Okta API + SCIM + OIDC + SAML + WebAuthn + SDKs) + `apigw` (per-org + OAuth + API-token + API-Access-Management) + `observability` (System Log + SIEM streaming + audit)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Workforce Identity Essentials → **Silver**; Professional → **Gold**; Enterprise + Identity Governance + Privileged Access + FedRAMP (full Workforce Identity Cloud — dedicated cell + 99.99%-SLA + EU-residency + FedRAMP-High + DOD-IL4 + HIPAA-BAA + Identity-Governance + Privileged-Access + Workflows + Customer-Managed-Keys + on-prem-agents + Customer-IP-Allowlist) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `cyber` (canonical security domain — CrowdStrike is the cloud-native endpoint-security category leader; differentiated by single-agent unified platform spanning endpoint + identity + cloud + log + attack-surface + threat-intel + managed-hunting + DLP under one Falcon Cloud) + `intelligence` (Charlotte AI + Adversary Intelligence + Indicator-of-Attack ML) + `observability` (LogScale log analytics + Falcon telemetry stream) + `compliance` (SOC-2 + FedRAMP-Moderate + DOD-IL4 + GDPR + HIPAA + PCI-DSS + ISO + EU-AI-Act for Charlotte) + `workflow-engine` (Falcon Foundry app builder + Fusion workflows) + `developer-sdk` (Falcon API + Falcon Foundry SDK + LogScale-Search SDK + CrowdStrike-CLI) + `apigw` (per-customer + OAuth-Client-Credentials + API-Client-ID + Secret) + `iam` (Falcon Identity Protection + Falcon ZTA)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Falcon Go (SMB) → **Silver**; Falcon Pro → **Gold**; Falcon Enterprise / Premium / Complete + FedRAMP (Falcon Enterprise + Falcon Complete managed-MDR + Falcon-OverWatch managed-threat-hunt + dedicated cluster + 99.95%-SLA + EU-residency + FedRAMP-Moderate + DOD-IL4 + Customer-Managed-Keys + Customer-Quarantine-Region) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `workflow-engine` (canonical work-management domain — Asana is the work-graph leader; differentiated by goals + portfolios + workload + rules + AI-summaries + flow-builder unified) + `intelligence` (Asana-AI Smart-fields + Smart-status + Smart-summary + Smart-goals + Smart-workflow) + `collaboration` (comments + inbox + status updates + proofing + approvals) + `automation` (Rules + Bundles + Flow + Triggers + Actions) + `apigw` (Asana REST + GraphQL + Webhooks + Asana Connect) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO-27001 + ISO-27017 + ISO-27018 + GDPR + HIPAA-eligible Enterprise + EU-DPF + EU-AI-Act for Asana-AI) + `developer-sdk` (Asana-API-protocol-compat + Forms + Rules-DSL)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Personal / Starter → **Silver**; Advanced → **Gold**; Enterprise / Enterprise+ (EKM + audit-log API + SAML-SSO + SCIM + cross-regional residency + HIPAA-BAA + service-accounts) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `workflow-engine` (canonical multi-product Work-OS — monday differs from Asana by flexible column-type system + apps-framework + multi-product unified) + `intelligence` (monday-AI Quick-Compose + Sentiment + Assistant + Workflows + Smart-Categories + AI-Block) + `automation` (250+ recipes + custom builder) + `apigw` (monday GraphQL API v2 + Webhooks) + `developer-sdk` (monday Apps Framework + monday-SDK-JS + monday Apps CLI) + `marketplace` (monday Apps marketplace) + `compliance` (SOC-2 + ISO-27001 / 27017 / 27018 / 27701 + GDPR + HIPAA-eligible Enterprise + EU-DPF + EU-AI-Act for monday-AI) + `collaboration` (updates + mentions + WorkdocsAI)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Free / Basic → **Silver**; Standard / Pro → **Gold**; Enterprise (SCIM + SAML + audit + HIPAA + IP-restrict + panic-mode + private-cloud option + 99.9%-SLA) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `iam` (canonical CIAM surface — Auth0 differs from Okta D-109 by CIAM-first vs workforce-first; Auth0 is Customer Identity, Okta is Workforce Identity) + `intelligence` (Adaptive-MFA risk-engine + Bot-Detection + Breached-Password) + `automation` (Actions JS/Node sandbox + Login-Flows) + `apigw` (Management-API + Authentication-API + OIDC + OAuth-2.0 + FAPI-2.0-Adv + SAML) + `data-store` (User-Store + Connection-Store) + `compliance` (SOC-2 + ISO-27001 + ISO-27018 + GDPR + HIPAA-BAA + PCI-DSS + EU-DPF + FedRAMP-Moderate + CSA-STAR + EU-AI-Act for risk-scoring) + `marketplace` (Auth0 Marketplace integrations) + `observability` (Log-Streams + Tenant-Logs + Real-Time-Webtask-Logs) + `policy` (ReBAC via Auth0 FGA Zanzibar)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Free (7K MAU + 25K MAU social) → **Silver**; Essentials (B2C $35/mo + B2B $150/mo) → **Gold**; Professional + Enterprise (HIPAA-eligible + private-cloud + 99.99%-SLA + FAPI-2.0-Adv + step-up + custom-domain + SSO-from-multiple-orgs + Adaptive-MFA + Bot-Detection-pro + Org-MAU 100M+) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `iam` (canonical multi-domain IAM — ForgeRock differs from Auth0 D-126 (CIAM) + Okta D-109 (Workforce) by being unified across Workforce + CIAM + IoT + B2B, plus self-managed deploy option) + `identity-governance` (Autonomous Identity ML access-review + IGA + Access-Certification) + `intelligence` (Autonomous Identity ML + risk-engine) + `automation` (Trees / Journeys auth-flow graph) + `apigw` (AM as authZ proxy + IG as identity-gateway) + `data-store` (DS as LDAP + custom-attribute schema) + `policy` (XACML + Authorization-Service + Policy-Sets + Policy-Conditions) + `compliance` (FedRAMP-Moderate + StateRAMP + HITRUST + SOC-2 + ISO-27001 + GDPR + HIPAA + CSA-STAR + EU-DPF + Common Criteria EAL-4+ + EU-AI-Act for Autonomous-Identity) + `iot` (IoT-Identity + Thing-SDK) + `verifiable-credentials` (DID + VC issuance)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Identity Cloud Essentials → **Silver**; Identity Cloud Standard → **Gold**; Identity Cloud Enterprise + Government Cloud + self-managed Platform with FedRAMP + Common-Criteria EAL-4+ + Autonomous-Identity ML + IoT + DID/VC + IGA + 99.99%-SLA → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `iam` (canonical multi-product IAM — Ping differs from Auth0/Okta/ForgeRock by federation-hub-first heritage + on-prem PingFederate legacy + cloud-native PingOne + DaVinci orchestrator) + `intelligence` (PingOne Risk + PingOne Protect ML fraud + PingIntelligence-for-APIs ML) + `automation` (DaVinci flow orchestration) + `apigw` (PingAccess + PingIntelligence-for-APIs) + `data-store` (PingDirectory + PingDataSync) + `policy` (PingAuthorize XACML+ABAC + PingDataGovernance) + `compliance` (FedRAMP-Moderate + StateRAMP + SOC-2 + ISO-27001 + ISO-27017 + ISO-27018 + GDPR + HIPAA + PCI-DSS + CSA-STAR + EU-DPF + EU-AI-Act for Risk/Protect/Intelligence) + `verifiable-credentials` (PingOne Neo wallet + VC) + `identity-verification` (PingOne Verify document + biometric)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: PingOne Essentials → **Silver**; PingOne Plus → **Gold**; PingOne Premium + on-prem PingFederate + PingAccess + PingDirectory + PingAuthorize + DaVinci + Neo + Verify + Risk + Protect + FedRAMP-Moderate + 99.99%-SLA → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `secrets-mgmt` (canonical password + secrets vault — 1Password differs from LastPass/Dashlane/Bitwarden by Secret-Key dual-factor + Connect + SCIM Bridge + Events API + Developer Tools depth + Kolide Device Trust integration) + `iam` (SCIM provisioning + Unlock-With-SSO + Service Accounts) + `automation` (Secrets-Automation via Connect + CLI + Service Accounts) + `observability` (Events API streaming to SIEM) + `apigw` (1Password Connect REST API + Service-Account access) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO-27001 / 27017 / 27018 / 27701 + GDPR + PCI-DSS + HIPAA-eligible Business + EU-DPF) + `device-trust` (Kolide endpoint-posture + device-trust enforcement) + `saas-discovery` (Trelica SaaS-discovery + governance + spend) + `developer-tools` (Shell-plugins + Git-credential + SSH-agent + ssh-config + 1Password-Operator for K8s)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Families / Personal → **Silver**; Teams / Teams-Starter → **Gold**; Business + Enterprise (SCIM + SSO-unlock + Events-API + Connect + Service-Accounts + Kolide + Trelica + advanced audit + custom-roles + Insights + 99.95%-SLA) → **Platinum**.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: B (capability fit on `secrets-mgmt` (canonical enterprise password manager — LastPass differs from 1Password D-129 by Federated-Login heritage + SSO+PWM bundling + AD-FS integration + post-breach-2022 trust-rebuild posture) + `iam` (Federated-Login + SSO + SCIM + AD-Connector) + `intelligence` (Security-Dashboard hygiene + Dark-Web-Monitoring) + `observability` (Activity-Reports + Splunk-Add-On + Reporting API) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + SOC-3 + ISO-27001 / 27017 / 27018 + GDPR + CCPA + BSI-C5 + post-breach-2022 commitments)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Free / Premium → **Bronze**; Teams → **Silver**; Business (with SSO + MFA + SCIM + AD-Connector + Activity-Reports + 99.9%-SLA) → **Gold** (intentionally NOT Platinum: 2022 breach history downgrades trust ceiling per industry consensus). Note: Some Oyatie tenants explicitly forbid LastPass per security policy — `compliance-pack: lastpass_forbidden` is a known tenant-side gate.
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- **Naming justification**: `secrets-mgmt` + `iam` + `intelligence` + `observability` + `compliance` are canonical 12-layer enum entries per ADR-0105 (13-value canonical); `lastpass` + `lastpass-business` + `lastpass-federation` + `lastpass-k1-k2` + `lastpass-sso` + `lastpass-mfa-api` + `lastpass-scim` + `lastpass-ad-connector` + `lastpass-authenticator` + `lastpass-emergency-access` + `lastpass-equivalent-domains` + `lastpass-security-dashboard` + `lastpass-dark-web-monitoring` + `lpass-cli` are benchmark alias-only. The `secrets-mgmt` µservice (per ADR-0131 per-microservice flat layout) is the canonical LastPass-equivalent surface, sharing target with 1Password D-129; LastPass distinguished by federated-login + SSO-bundling + AD-FS heritage. Tier downgrade to Gold (NOT Platinum) reflects 2022-breach-induced industry trust ceiling per [[no-silent-regression]].
docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `cloud-security` (canonical CNAPP — Wiz differs from CrowdStrike D-110 / Defender D-047 / Sentinel D-048 by agentless-first via cloud API + Security-Graph unified inventory across multi-cloud + toxic-combination + attack-path analytics) + `intelligence` (Threat-Center + ML toxic-combination + Attack-Path scoring + AI-SPM model-risk) + `automation` (Workflows + Webhooks + Auto-Remediation) + `policy` (Wiz Controls — custom-policy framework + Compliance-Frameworks 100+) + `apigw` (Wiz GraphQL API + REST API) + `developer-tools` (Wiz CLI + Wiz Code IDE integrations + Pre-commit hooks + Pull-Request annotations) + `data-store` (DSPM data-inventory) + `iam` (CIEM entitlement-analysis) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO-27001 + GDPR + FedRAMP-Moderate-in-process + 100+ frameworks rendered as Compliance-Posture reports) + `observability` (Cloud-Events firehose + Audit-Log)). Capability-tier mapping per ADR-0316: Wiz Essential → **Gold**; Wiz Advanced + Wiz Enterprise (with Wiz Code + Runtime-Sensor + Defend + AI-SPM + DSPM + Outpost + 99.9%-SLA + private-cloud) → **Platinum**.

docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `developer-security` (canonical secrets-detection — GitGuardian differs from Snyk D-132 by secrets-detection-first depth + honeytoken-decoys + NHI-governance + 450+ detectors + post-detection auto-revoke + pre-receive-hook for shift-left) + `intelligence` (detector ML + validity-check + severity-scoring) + `automation` (auto-revoke + auto-healing-playbooks + webhook-actions) + `apigw` (GitGuardian-API REST) + `developer-tools` (ggshield CLI + IDE-plugin + pre-commit + CI integrations) + `policy` (custom-detector + ignore-rules + secret-classification policies) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO-27001 + GDPR + EU-Residency) + `iam` (NHI Governance — non-human-identity lifecycle)). docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `artifact-repository` (canonical binary repo manager — Sonatype Nexus differs from JFrog Artifactory by Maven-Java-heritage + open-source-tier broad adoption + Sonatype-IQ-Server SCA integration) + `developer-security` (Nexus IQ Server SCA + Lifecycle + Firewall + Auditor + Container + Lift) + `intelligence` (Sonatype Intelligence + OSS-Index + research-grade vuln-DB) + `policy` (Nexus IQ Policies + Routing-Rules + Cleanup-Policies + Firewall-Rules) + `automation` (Auto-Release + Cleanup + Tasks) + `apigw` (REST-API + Groovy-Provisioning-API + Repository-Indexers) + `developer-tools` (Maven plugin + Gradle plugin + npm publish + Docker push) + `compliance` (SOC-2 + ISO-27001 + Common Criteria EAL-2 + FedRAMP-Moderate hosted via Sonatype Cloud + SBOM CycloneDX + SPDX export) + `data-store` (Blob-Stores + Components + Asset metadata)). docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `data-store` (hybrid file-share leader — Egnyte differentiated by hybrid cloud + on-prem CIFS/NFS appliance + industry-vertical packs + Protect DLP + Smart Cache) + `intelligence` (Egnyte AI Copilot + Knowledge + Document + Smart Search) + `policy` (Protect — DLP + Smart Classification + Ransomware Detection + Supervised Access) + `automation` (Workflows + Tasks + Webform) + `apigw` (Egnyte Public API v1/v2 + Webhooks + WebDAV + FTP) + `compliance` (SOC-2 Type II + ISO-27001 + GDPR + HIPAA-BAA + 21 CFR Part 11 + GxP + FINRA + SEC-17a-4 + FedRAMP-Moderate + EU-AI-Act for Egnyte-AI) + `developer-sdk` (REST API + WebDAV + FTP + Drive-SDK) + `marketplace` (Egnyte App Gallery)). docs/decisions/ADR-0321-b2b-saas-industry-leader-coverage.md:- Coverage tier: A (capability fit on `web` (broadest no-code website platform — Wix differentiated by Wix Studio for agencies + Wix Velo for devs + 8+ business verticals + AI Site Generator + Wix Mobile App) + `commerce` (Wix Stores + Bookings + Restaurants + Hotels + Events) + `marketing` (Wix Email + SMS + Inbox CRM + Marketing Suite) + `scheduling` (Wix Bookings) + `intelligence` (Wix ADI + AI Site Generator + AI Content) + `apigw` (Wix REST API + GraphQL + Webhooks + Wix Velo serverless) + `developer-sdk` (Wix Velo + Wix-JS + Wix CLI + Custom Elements) + `marketplace` (Wix App Market + Wix Marketplace designers) + `compliance` (SOC-2 + ISO-27001 + GDPR + CCPA + PCI-DSS + EU-AI-Act for AI features)). docs/decisions/ADR-0344-sustainability-finops-dimensional-model.md:- Opt-in (paid tenants that purchase the carbon-cost-passthrough product): `co2_grams_sum_in_period * carbon_cost_per_gram_usd`, where `carbon_cost_per_gram_usd` is per-jurisdiction-published (EU ETS price; California Cap-and-Trade; voluntary market via Verra or Gold Standard).

docs/decisions/ADR-0195-stream-processing-tier.md:Flink is the community standard for class B (stateful streaming) workloads. AWS, Google, Microsoft, Oracle all offer managed Flink (AWS Managed Service for Apache Flink, Google Cloud Dataflow built on Apache Beam + Flink runner, Azure Stream Analytics is Microsoft-specific but Azure also offers managed Flink, Oracle GoldenGate Stream Analytics). No hyperscaler has rebuilt Flink in-house; oyatie's posture follows the same trajectory — keep Flink for the escalation tier.
docs/decisions/ADR-0195-stream-processing-tier.md:- **Oracle GoldenGate Stream Analytics** — proprietary engine; in-house at Oracle for the in-CDC streaming slot.
docs/decisions/ADR-0338-pod-runtime-tier-0-to-3.md:The ADR-0254 invariant ("K8s + Cloud Hypervisor + Kata pods") was authored before the cellular tier numbering convention from ADR-0248 was finalized and before the tenant_class model from ADR-0330 collapsed the prior Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum tier ladder. The implicit reading of ADR-0254 was "Kata everywhere." Capacity modeling against that reading shows the following overhead:
docs/decisions/ADR-0338-pod-runtime-tier-0-to-3.md:The numbering decision was deliberately reversed from the early Oyatie capability-tier draft (which used Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum ascending). The Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum tier ladder is retired per ADR-0329 + `feedback_no_capability_tiers_2026_05_20`; the pod runtime tier axis uses the ADR-0248 convention without reintroducing the ascending-prestige semantics of the retired ladder.
docs/decisions/ADR-0338-pod-runtime-tier-0-to-3.md:  - ADR-0329 (tier system retirement; Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum retired; this ADR does not reintroduce the retired ladder semantics).

docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Bronze exists.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Silver exists.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Gold exists.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Platinum exists.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Golden signals are represented per tier.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Per-tier deltas now describe what changes from Bronze to Silver to Gold to Platinum.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:The four tiers are Bronze.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:The four tiers include Silver.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:The four tiers include Gold.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:The four tiers include Platinum.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:- j166: Mira Goldberg strategic acquisition go/no-go shows CSO planning, diligence handoffs, restricted deal workspaces, and information barriers.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Bronze maps bounded SMB or pre-production activation.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Silver maps single-region high-availability production activation.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Gold maps multi-AZ revenue production activation.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Platinum maps sovereign or regulated pack-bound activation.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Golden signals per tier exist as a reusable reliability reference.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum should become grantable and revocable capabilities.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Evidence point 008: Bronze is the bounded activation story.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Evidence point 009: Silver is the high-availability production story.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Evidence point 010: Gold is the revenue-grade multi-AZ story.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Evidence point 011: Platinum is the regulated or sovereign story.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Safe claim 004: Oyatie uses Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum capability tiers.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Runtime proof 005: tier grant command validates Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum ids.
docs/architecture/wave-3-g-executive-briefing-2026-05-20-post-remediation.md:Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum tiers now map across services and vendors.

docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:5. Audit-chain: a sealed audit event records `(tenant_id, role_projection_id, capability_tier_id, workflow_run_id, ontology_object_ref, policy_fragment_version, audit_chain_event_id)`.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:Verify context surfaces the current `(tenant_id, role_projection_id, capability_tier_id, workspace, locale, compliance_pack)` tuple to the user before a high-stakes action. The widget is the same icon, the same keyboard shortcut, and the same color treatment in every product surface.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:- 07:00 verify context against the assigned patient list at handoff. The substrate displays `(tenant=teaching-hospital, role=registered-nurse, capability_tier=clinical-bedside, workspace=med-surg-4w, locale=en-US, pack=clinical-HIPAA-US)`.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:- Verify context at clock-in against the inbound dock assignment. Substrate displays `(tenant=fulfillment-LLC, role=receiving-clerk, capability_tier=logistics-inbound, workspace=dock-bay-7, locale=en-US, pack=logistics-product-safety-US)`.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:- Verify context daily at studio open; substrate displays `(tenant=studio-LLC, role=owner-operator, capability_tier=services-appointment, workspace=studio-floor, locale=en-US, pack=small-business-cosmetology-US)`.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:- Verify context at clock-in; substrate displays `(tenant=sustainment-site, role=maintenance-technician, capability_tier=depot-maintenance, workspace=line-bay-3, locale=en-US, pack=defense-CMMC-ITAR-EAR-US)`.
docs/architecture/training-cost-doctrine-2026-05-21.md:The substrate counts Cedar denials whose root cause is `tenant_mismatch`, `role_mismatch`, `capability_tier_mismatch`, `workspace_mismatch`, or `pack_mismatch`. The signal is broken out by user, role projection, and time.

docs/api/drive-api-reference.md:| Bronze | 100 rps | 30 rps | 250 MiB/s | 500 MiB/s | dev and demo cells |
docs/api/drive-api-reference.md:| Silver | 1,000 rps | 300 rps | 2 GiB/s | 5 GiB/s | default tenant tier |
docs/api/drive-api-reference.md:| Gold | 5,000 rps | 1,500 rps | 10 GiB/s | 25 GiB/s | multi-AZ high-volume |
docs/api/drive-api-reference.md:| Platinum | 10,000 rps | 3,000 rps | 20 GiB/s | 50 GiB/s | sovereign pack in-pack only |
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:| Bronze | 40 | 80 | 100 | 2x for 10s |
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:| Silver | 120 | 240 | 500 | 2x for 20s |
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:| Gold | 350 | 700 | 2000 | 3x for 30s |
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:| Platinum | 1000 | 2000 | 8000 | 3x for 60s |
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:- Emit ingestion: Bronze `250 events/s`, Silver `1000`, Gold `5000`, Platinum `20000`.
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:- Proof lookup: Bronze `25 rps`, Silver `100`, Gold `400`, Platinum `1500`.
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:- Export requests: Bronze `1 rps`, Silver `3`, Gold `10`, Platinum `25`.
docs/api/audit-chain-api-reference.md:- Integrity replay: Bronze `1 concurrent`, Silver `3`, Gold `10`, Platinum `25`.
docs/api/governance-api-reference.md:| Bronze | 30 | 60 | 50 | 2x for 10s |

docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:# Upgrade A Tenant From Bronze To Platinum
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:You will upgrade the Contoso Research work tenant from the Bronze capability tier to the Platinum capability tier without creating a product-specific fork. The finished tenant will have the Platinum grant active, the Bronze grant superseded, the new permit set evaluated through Cedar, the upgraded ontology projections visible in the UX shell, the required workflow templates loaded, and the audit-chain evidence packet ready for customer-success handoff.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.grant.read`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.grant.create`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.grant.activate`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.grant.supersede`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.projection.preview`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.projection.apply`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.workflow.load`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `capability_tier.ux.render`.

docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Named Cedar permit: `audit.capability_tier.export`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Billing authority: Li must approve the projected Platinum monthly cost before activation.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Evidence destination: `Drive -> Tenant Evidence -> Tier Upgrades -> 2026-05-20 Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Verification query name: `tutorial.capability_tier_upgrade_status`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:- Stop condition: Platinum is active, Bronze is superseded, no product fork exists, audit export is sealed.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm the visible tier card reads `Bronze`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   In the modal titled `Start tier upgrade`, choose target tier `Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   The workspace banner should read `Draft upgrade: Bronze -> Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:3. Confirm the Bronze grant before changing anything.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Save the export to Drive path `/Tenant Evidence/Tier Upgrades/2026-05-20 Platinum/source-bronze-snapshot.json`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:4. Preview Platinum entitlement changes.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Choose `Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   The expected result is `allow` for `capability_tier.grant.activate`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   The expected result is `allow` for `capability_tier.workflow.load`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   The expected result is `allow` for `capability_tier.projection.apply`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm the context includes `tier_target=Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Export the decision log to `/Tenant Evidence/Tier Upgrades/2026-05-20 Platinum/cedar-preview.json`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Save to `/Tenant Evidence/Tier Upgrades/2026-05-20 Platinum/workflow-template-preview.json`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm the current Bronze monthly estimate is `USD 240.00`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm the Platinum monthly estimate is `USD 1,840.00`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Add note `Approve Platinum upgrade for enterprise workflow and intelligence pilot`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Set approval reason to `Activate Platinum capability tier for Contoso Research enterprise rollout`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Enter comment `Approved for tenant-wide Platinum capability activation`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:12. Activate Platinum.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   In the `Activation` tab, select `Activate Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   The confirmation modal title should read `Activate Platinum for Contoso Research`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Check `Bronze grant will be superseded`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Check `Platinum grant will become active`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Wait until the progress step `Bronze grant superseded` is complete.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:13. Confirm Bronze is superseded and Platinum is active.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm the tier card now reads `Platinum`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:14. Check the Platinum UX shell.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Confirm audit event `PlatinumCapabilityPreviewed`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Include `Source Bronze snapshot`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Save to `/Tenant Evidence/Tier Upgrades/2026-05-20 Platinum/evidence-packet.json`.
docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:   Search for `tutorial.capability_tier_upgrade_status`.

docs/tutorials/capability-tier-upgrade-bronze-to-platinum.md:Run the named query `tutorial.capability_tier_upgrade_status`.

docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:## 04 - Bronze Tier Economics
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target customer: founder-led SMB, small regulated team, or department pilot.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze list ARR: $48,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze monthly equivalent: $4,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze onboarding fee: $15,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze included users: 50 named users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze additional user pack: $6,000 ARR per 25 users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze included workflows: 10 active workflows.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze included evidence volume: 100,000 audit events per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze included integration calls: 100,000 calls per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze included AI budget: $1,000 model/provider cost per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target cloud cost: $900 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target support cost: $500 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target infrastructure plus support cost: $16,800 annually.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target gross profit: $31,200 annually.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target gross margin: 65 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze CAC target after repeatability: $12,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze CAC payback target: 4.6 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze early CAC risk: $25,000 if founder-led sales is not repeatable.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze early CAC payback at $25,000 CAC: 9.6 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze expansion path: Silver upgrade within 12 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze downgrade rule: preserve data export, evidence archive, and policy history.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze risk: support burden exceeds ACV.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze mitigation: product-led templates before broad SMB launch.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze competitor names: HubSpot Starter/Professional, Zoho One, Monday.com, ClickUp, Google Workspace, Microsoft 365.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze moat factor: governed workflow and evidence in an affordable package.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze milestone: SMB beta decision by 2027-04-30.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:## 05 - Silver Tier Economics
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target customer: midmarket team, regulated department, or professional-services firm.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver list ARR: $180,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver monthly equivalent: $15,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver onboarding fee: $50,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver included users: 250 named users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver additional user pack: $18,000 ARR per 100 users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver included workflows: 50 active workflows.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver included evidence volume: 1.0 million audit events per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver included integration calls: 1.0 million calls per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver included AI budget: $5,000 provider cost per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target cloud cost: $3,500 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target support cost: $2,500 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target infrastructure plus support cost: $72,000 annually.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target gross profit: $108,000 annually.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target gross margin: 60 percent early, 72 percent at scale.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver CAC target: $60,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver CAC payback at 60 percent gross margin: 6.7 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver CAC payback at 72 percent gross margin: 5.6 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver expansion path: Gold upgrade, evidence-pack add-on, integration overage.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver risk: customer expects enterprise controls at midmarket price.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver mitigation: clear tier limits and compliance-pack upgrade triggers.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver competitor names: Salesforce Professional/Enterprise, ServiceNow Pro editions, Workday departmental adoption, Microsoft Dynamics 365, Atlassian Premium.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver moat factor: dual-context professional collaboration plus evidence.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver milestone: first Silver pilot template by 2027-01-31.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:## 06 - Gold Tier Economics
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target customer: enterprise business unit, regulated operator, or enterprise SaaS company.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold list ARR: $720,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold monthly equivalent: $60,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold onboarding fee: $150,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold included users: 1,500 named users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold additional user pack: $60,000 ARR per 500 users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold included workflows: 250 active workflows.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold included evidence volume: 10.0 million audit events per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold included integration calls: 10.0 million calls per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold included AI budget: $25,000 provider cost per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target cloud cost: $15,000 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target support cost: $12,000 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target customer engineering cost allocation: $8,000 per month after onboarding.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target annual service cost: $420,000 early.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target gross profit early: $300,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target gross margin early: 41.7 percent if heavily supported.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target annual service cost at scale: $180,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target gross profit at scale: $540,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target gross margin at scale: 75 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold CAC target by year 3: $240,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold CAC payback at scale gross margin: 5.3 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold early CAC risk: $500,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold early CAC payback at 60 percent gross margin: 13.9 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold expansion path: Platinum, dedicated cell, evidence packs, AI usage, marketplace packs.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold competitor names: Salesforce Enterprise/Unlimited, ServiceNow Enterprise, SAP BTP plus modules, Workday Enterprise, Microsoft Dynamics 365 Enterprise.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold moat factor: governed enterprise workflow with audit-chain evidence.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold milestone: first paid Gold pilot by 2026-12-15.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:## 07 - Platinum Tier Economics
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target customer: regulated multinational, sovereign workload, or high-assurance enterprise.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum list ARR: $1,800,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum monthly equivalent: $150,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum onboarding fee: $400,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum included users: 5,000 named users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum additional user pack: $150,000 ARR per 1,000 users.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum included workflows: 1,000 active workflows.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum included evidence volume: 100.0 million audit events per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum included integration calls: 50.0 million calls per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum included AI budget: $75,000 provider cost per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target cloud cost: $60,000 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target support cost: $35,000 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target compliance support allocation: $20,000 per month.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target annual service cost: $1,380,000 early.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target gross profit early: $420,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target gross margin early: 23.3 percent if underpriced or bespoke.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target annual service cost at scale: $540,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target gross profit at scale: $1,260,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target gross margin at scale: 70 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum dedicated cell add-on improves margin when priced above $750,000 ARR.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum CAC target by year 3: $600,000.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum CAC payback at 70 percent gross margin: 5.7 months.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum early CAC risk: $1.2 million.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum risk: bespoke regulated implementation destroys product margin.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum mitigation: sell only after security baseline and reusable pack templates.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum competitor names: Palantir Foundry, SAP regulated deployments, Oracle Cloud and Fusion, Microsoft Government/regulated cloud, ServiceNow regulated workflows.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum moat factor: compliance packs plus dedicated cells plus audit-chain evidence.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum milestone: first Platinum-ready compliance pack by 2028-03-31.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Standard managed cell is included in Bronze, Silver, and Gold.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze migration package: $75,000 one-time.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver migration package: $150,000 one-time.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold migration package: $250,000 one-time.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum migration package: $500,000 one-time.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze target gross margin at scale: 65 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver target gross margin at scale: 72 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold target gross margin at scale: 75 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum target gross margin at scale: 70 percent.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Example Bronze to Silver expansion: $48,000 to $180,000 ARR.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Example Silver to Gold expansion: $180,000 to $720,000 ARR.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Example Gold to Platinum expansion: $720,000 to $1,800,000 ARR.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Procurement argument: Gold at $720,000 ARR is cheaper than multiple overlapping enterprise systems.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Procurement argument: Platinum at $1.8 million ARR is justified only with compliance and deployment value.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Procurement argument: Bronze at $48,000 ARR competes with bundled SMB suites only if governance matters.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Bronze at $48,000 ARR creates an accessible entry point.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Silver at $180,000 ARR creates midmarket and departmental adoption.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Gold at $720,000 ARR is the main enterprise wedge.
docs/investor/unit-economics-and-pricing-model.md:- Platinum at $1,800,000 ARR is the regulated and sovereign expansion tier.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Bronze defines the entry tier for simple tenants.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Silver defines the managed-growth tier for more workflow depth.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Gold defines the regulated-enterprise tier for larger organizations.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Platinum defines the dedicated-cell and highest-assurance tier.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- The moat is not the names Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Bronze at $48,000 ARR is an entry tenant price.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Silver at $180,000 ARR is a department or regulated-growth price.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Gold at $720,000 ARR is a serious enterprise-platform price.
docs/investor/competitive-landscape-and-positioning.md:- Platinum at $1,800,000 ARR is a dedicated-cell regulated-enterprise price.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Platinum create commercial packaging.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Bronze can map to small-team or starter use.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Silver can map to professional collaboration.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Gold can map to enterprise automation and integration.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Platinum can map to regulated or sovereign posture.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Compliance packs can push tier selection toward Gold or Platinum.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Platinum regulated tier is modeled at $1,800,000 ARR before cell add-ons.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- First dedicated-cell architecture proof should be ready before Platinum sales.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Defense strategy: make Bronze and Silver tiers usable.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Oyatie's Gold model at $720,000 ARR is 1.29 percent of $55.7 million.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Oyatie's Platinum model at $1.8 million ARR is 3.23 percent of $55.7 million.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- Example Gold subscription: $720,000.
docs/investor/moat-and-defensibility.md:- The expansion path is Bronze to Silver to Gold to Platinum.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- The round should support dedicated-cell proof before Platinum-tier sales.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- Security work is required before claiming Platinum readiness.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- Tranche 3 stop condition: do not sell Platinum before dedicated-cell and security evidence exist.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- Average mature Gold-tier ACV target: $720,000.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- Platinum dedicated-cell ACV target: $1.8 million before add-ons.
docs/investor/ask-and-use-of-funds.md:- Governance principle: no Platinum sale without security and dedicated-cell evidence.

docs/user-journeys/j166-cso-mira-goldberg-strategic-acquisition-go-no-go/README.md:# j166 — CSO Mira Goldberg drives a $186M acquisition go/no-go decision in 9 days
docs/user-journeys/j166-cso-mira-goldberg-strategic-acquisition-go-no-go/README.md:Mira Goldberg (מירה גולדברג) is a **39-year-old Chief Strategy Officer** at **Skylark Logistics Solutions Inc.**, a Boston-headquartered last-mile supply-chain SaaS company (~480 employees, $148M ARR, Series D, profitable since 2025-Q2). Mira is American-Israeli (dual citizen; mother from Tel Aviv), born in Newton MA in 1987, MBA Wharton 2014, joined Skylark from a Big 4 strategy consulting practice in 2023-09. She reports to the CEO Adrian Cheng-Whitford and to the Strategy + M&A committee chair Director Hannah Beauregard.
docs/user-journeys/j166-cso-mira-goldberg-strategic-acquisition-go-no-go/README.md:Mira Goldberg is **MASTER-ROSTER §3.4 row 234** — the canonical CSO persona at a mid-market growth-stage B2B SaaS company driving M&A. This persona covers ~6,800 CSO-class roles globally in tech-enabled growth companies (BLS 2024 code 11-1011 narrowed to "Chief Strategy"). M&A decision support is one of the most consequential workflows a CSO drives; getting it wrong destroys hundreds of millions in shareholder value.
docs/user-journeys/j166-cso-mira-goldberg-strategic-acquisition-go-no-go/story.md:Spring is on. 14°C. The Seaport harbor is calm under thin overcast. The willows along Pier 4 have leafed out. Mira Goldberg badges into the 22nd-floor Skylark executive suite at 07:42:14 EDT. She is wearing a navy blazer + slim charcoal trousers + the cobalt fountain pen she uses for paper signatures (decreasingly relevant in a passkey-first workplace; she keeps it for emotional ballast).
docs/user-journeys/j166-cso-mira-goldberg-strategic-acquisition-go-no-go/ux-flow.md:│  M&A WORKSPACE · Skylark Logistics · CSO Mira Goldberg                   │
docs/user-journeys/j175-aanya-kapoor-LP-portfolio-tax-and-K1-distribution/schemas/cedar-policy.cedar:// Deployed to: aanya-kapoor-personal-2008 + 4 fund-GP tenants + IRS + 8 state revenue + Wells Goldman CPA tenant
docs/user-journeys/j175-aanya-kapoor-LP-portfolio-tax-and-K1-distribution/README.md:It is **Wednesday May 20, 2027, 19:48 PDT** (Pacific Daylight Time). Aanya is at her dining-room table in their Noe Valley home (4-bedroom Victorian, purchased 2021). The kids are asleep; Vikram is in Tel Aviv on a 2-week LP-relations trip for a Sequoia portfolio company. Aanya has set aside Wednesday + Thursday + Friday evenings to work through her **2026 LP K-1 distributions** (her FY2026 final K-1s arrived from all 4 funds between April 14 and May 12, 2027). She has 11 days until her CPA's filing deadline (June 1; her CPA Mrs. Patricia Wells-Goldman at Wells Goldman & Associates files her return).

docs/user-journeys/j175-aanya-kapoor-LP-portfolio-tax-and-K1-distribution/story.md:She authenticates her work laptop (a 16" M4 MacBook Pro): passkey + face attestation + her **accredited investor + qualified purchaser** attestation token (issued by Wells Goldman & Associates her CPA firm 6 months ago after their annual KYC update).
docs/user-journeys/j175-aanya-kapoor-LP-portfolio-tax-and-K1-distribution/story.md:Sunday evening. Aanya packages everything for her CPA Patricia Wells-Goldman.
docs/user-journeys/j175-aanya-kapoor-LP-portfolio-tax-and-K1-distribution/integration-test-plan.md:Intern-buildable plan: stand up Aanya's personal tenant + 4 fund GP tenants + IRS tenant + 8 state revenue tenants + Wells Goldman CPA tenant; mock 4 K-1 PDFs with deterministic line items; mock LP capital account reconciliation; mock tax-character categorization; mock state apportionment; mock foreign tax credit; mock AMT + 1411 NIIT + 199A; mock quarterly estimated tax payments; mock WORM archival; mock GP-LP communication channel; seed 10 pack overlays; seed Cedar bundle.

docs/standards/hyperscaler-best-practices.md:**SRE practice: error budgets + 4 golden signals.** SLO drives release decisions: if the error budget is healthy → ship; if depleted → freeze and stabilize. The four golden signals — latency, traffic, errors, saturation — are the canonical dashboard. Burn-rate alerts (1× = on-target, 3× = exhaust budget in 1/3 of window) are now standard. ([Google SRE — Monitoring Distributed Systems](https://sre.google/sre-book/monitoring-distributed-systems/); [Google SRE Workbook — Error Budget Policy](https://sre.google/workbook/error-budget-policy/); [Splunk — Four Golden Signals](https://www.splunk.com/en_us/blog/learn/sre-metrics-four-golden-signals-of-monitoring.html))

docs/plans/rename-plan-v4-clean-arch-2026-05-13.md:| **R1 — In-flight branch conflicts.** | M | M | Single 48 h coordination window via grit claim's exclusive symbol lock (not a separate fitness lane primitive; ADR-0054's existing icm-coordination-lock fallback covers the rename-event case per the same-commit ADR-0054 amendment). `git rerere` enabled on merge queue. Hybrid-C-Lite escape hatch (per ADR-0057) if 48 h window unschedulable within 2 weeks of Shard 0 merge. |
docs/plans/rename-plan-v4-clean-arch-2026-05-13.md:| **R5 — Security P0 expedite during freeze window.** | L | H | Use **grit claim's normal authority** (no separate `expedite_override_token` machinery). If a P0 security hotfix must merge during the 48 h coordination window, the on-call security council member releases the grit symbol-lock via `grit done --agent <id> --force`, lands the P0, and re-acquires the lock. ICM rationale row at `direct-tool-invocations` topic. No fitness-lane primitive needed; grit's existing authority chain handles it. ADR-0057 §"Rollback/expedite protocol" documents the precondition list (same shape as v3 ADR-0055 §"Rollback/expedite protocol" but the token mechanism is replaced by grit claim's exclusive-lock authority). |
docs/plans/rename-plan-v4-clean-arch-2026-05-13.md:| **R10 — Bounded-context naming drift (NEW; B4 5-layer parity with R4).** Without a closed enum, two teams could disagree on the bounded context for the same domain (`audit` vs. `audit-chain`; `eventing` vs. `events`). | M | H | **Permanent-controls ledger (5 layers, matching R4 [lib] drift)**: (1) **Preflight**: xtask `--check-bc` (A4 codification check) runs before any crate scaffold; refuses unregistered BC. (2) **Ledger**: MISTAKES-LEDGER row keyed `BC-DRIFT-001` (template: "BC X added without rationale paragraph or owner field — corrected via ADR-0056 amendment"). (3) **Lane**: `oya-check-bounded-contexts` (LEAN-A2; renamed from iter-1-fold-A's singular `oya-check-bounded-context-registry` per Codex iter-2 stale-name fix at this R10 row) is BLOCKER post-§8.2 flip. (4) **ICM**: `icm store -t bc-drift-prevention -c "<scaffold-claim-for-new-bc-X>" -i critical -k "bc=X,scaffolder=<agent>"` before any new BC's first use. (5) **Citation probe**: `cargo doc --workspace --no-deps` references the registry; missing-BC reference fails docgen. 90-day auto-deprecation for zero-crate contexts. Scenario A pre-mortem mitigation. ADR-0056 §"Bounded context registry as a living document" + §"BC arbitrator" (B3) govern arbitration. |

docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:12. **Pragmatic git/gh** — permitted when genuine need + documented rationale via `icm store -t direct-tool-invocations`.
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:- **Grit-claimable symbols enumerated**: each new Rust crate exposes its public API via documented `pub` declarations indexable by `grit symbols`.
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:- **Icm-store payload**: each phase emits a documented `icm store -t <topic> -c <content>` event at completion (named in the phase Implementation Plan).
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:> "Any operator (agent or human) may invoke git/gh when (a) no grit/icm primitive exists for the operation AND (b) inventing one would be over-engineering AND (c) rationale is documented via `icm store -t direct-tool-invocations -c '<why>' -i critical` BEFORE invocation."
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:The audit trail requirement is the load-bearing piece: every direct git/gh invocation during the cutover MUST have a matching icm-store record. The `oya-governance-banned-primitives` lane (P5) MUST catch UNDOCUMENTED invocations, not all invocations. Specifically, the lane:
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:- Greps agent-instruction sections for `\bgit\b`, `\bgh\b`, `rtk git`, `rtk gh`.
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:- Cross-references each match against `icm recall -t direct-tool-invocations` for a same-day rationale.
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:- Fails CI only if a match has no corresponding icm record OR the icm record is older than 24h.
docs/plans/cutover-cross-cutting-amendments-2026-05-12.md:This satisfies the spec's §Constraints item 1 ("Sanctioned primitives are `{grit, icm, oya-tooling-agent-read}` only") by treating `{git, gh}` as documented-exception primitives rather than wholly-banned ones.
