docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:> **Status:** Accepted. **Owner:** `axis-foundry`. **Date:** 2026-05-12. **Governed by:** [ADR-0055](../../decisions/ADR-0055-four-layer-branch-pipeline.md). **Sanctioned primitives:** [ADR-0053](../../decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md). **Supersedes:** [ADR-0041](../../decisions/ADR-0041-gitops-trunk-based-and-release-branch-cut-at-tag.md).
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:| 0 | per-agent worktree (`.grit/worktrees/<agent-id>/`) | the agent doing the work | n/a (private workspace) | only the agent itself |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:| 1 | agent local dev clone (the agent's local copy of `origin/dev`) | `grit done --agent <id>` (atomic, treats as sync with origin/dev) | **none** — autonomous sync | only the agent itself |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:    W["agent worktree<br/>(.grit/worktrees/<id>/)"]
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:  W -->|"grit done<br/>(autonomous)"| LD
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:2. **Reviewer-agent verdict = `APPROVE`.** Per-change-class dispatch (per `docs/AGENTS.md`): `rust-reviewer`, `typescript-reviewer`, `python-reviewer`, `database-reviewer`, `security-reviewer`, `privacy-reviewer`, `tdd-guide`, `silent-failure-hunter`, `doc-updater`, `doc-style-reviewer`, `capability-reviewer`, `perf-reviewer`. Multi-class PRs invoke multiple reviewers in parallel; all must `APPROVE`. Verdict recorded via `icm store -t pr-review-verdicts -c '<verdict + items>' -i high -k 'pr-<id>,<reviewer>'` per [ADR-0053](../../decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md). Planned advisory lane: `oya-governance-pr-review-verdict-present` (planned blocker, scoped to this transition).
docs/release/branch-pipeline/branch-pipeline-architecture.md:- [ADR-0053](../../decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md) — all promoter agents use `grit` + `icm` + `oya-tooling-agent-read` exclusively; reviewer verdicts stored via `icm store -t pr-review-verdicts`.
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:  Per-stage rollback mechanics. local-dev: discard via grit cancel. origin/dev: revert
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:> **Status:** Accepted. **Owner:** `axis-foundry`. **Date:** 2026-05-12. **Governed by:** [ADR-0055](../../decisions/ADR-0055-four-layer-branch-pipeline.md). **Sanctioned primitives:** [ADR-0053](../../decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md).
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:**Mechanism.** `grit cancel --agent <id>` (or `grit discard`). Worktree state cleared; symbols released back to the claim pool.
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:**Evidence.** Audit event `EVT-WORKTREE-DISCARDED` emitted via `icm store -t worktree-events -c '<id, reason>' -i low`. No D14 evidence required (private workspace).
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:**Mechanism.** `git reset --hard origin/dev` (Directive 12 permitted with `icm store -t direct-tool-invocations -c 'rollback local-dev clone after grit cancel' -i high -k 'git,rollback'`). Or `grit cancel` if the agent had `grit done` to local-dev but not yet opened a PR.
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:| worktree discard | `EVT-WORKTREE-DISCARDED` | agent identity | `icm: worktree-events` |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:| local-dev reset | `EVT-LOCAL-DEV-RESET` | agent identity | `icm: local-dev-events` |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:| origin/dev revert | `revert_record` | reviewer-agent + dev-promoter | `audit-chain` + `icm: dev-promotions` |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:| staging revert (via origin/dev) | same as above + `EVT-STAGING-PROMOTED` | reviewer + dev-promoter + staging-promoter | `audit-chain` + `icm: staging-promotions` |
docs/release/branch-pipeline/rollback-mechanics-per-stage.md:| prod SLO-auto-rollback | `auto_rollback_record` | rollout-controller identity | `audit-chain` + `icm: prod-rollbacks` |

docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:# Deep-Dive Spec: oyatie Single Source of Truth + grit/icm agentic pipeline
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:Captured 2026-05-12. Output of `/deep-dive` Phase 4 crystallization. Feeds `/ralplan --consensus --direct` then `/autopilot` or `/team` under the grit `claim → work → done` lifecycle.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:Establish two cleanly-separated layers as oyatie's single source of truth, with every authoritative artifact tracked in the repo and every agent operation routed through `grit` (coordination) and `icm` (memory) without direct `git` or `gh` invocation.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:**Layer 2 — Agentic-pipeline contract.** `grit` is the sole sanctioned primitive for agent state transitions (`claim --agent --intent`, `worktree`, `done`, `session`, `assign`, `heartbeat`, `gc`). `icm` is the sole sanctioned primitive for cross-session knowledge (`recall`, `recall-context`, `store`, `update`, `health`). For read-side operations grit does not natively cover (commit history, branch diffs, PR comments), a thin sanctioned read-only helper CLI (`oya-agent-read`) wraps `git`/`gh` for agents — these helpers are the *only* non-grit/icm path agents may invoke. Direct agent-side `git` and `gh` are banned. The current orchestration glue (`bominal/agents/ultragoal/{ledger.jsonl, goals.json, codex-goal-*.json, G004-*, PAUSE.md}`, `omx ultragoal checkpoint/complete-goals`, `.codex/worktree_init.sh`, RTK `git`/`gh` references in agent-facing memory) is archived and deleted.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:1. **Sanctioned primitives are `grit` + `icm` + `oya-agent-read` only.** Any new agent-callable tool must justify itself against "why doesn't `grit` or `icm` already do this." Agent-side `git`/`gh` is banned.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:3. **Inventory before deletion.** No file or script is deleted until it appears in the inventory ledger at `oyatie/docs/decisions/registry/placeholder-debt/adr-follow-ups.yaml#grit-cutover-inventory (superseded by ADR-0116)` (or equivalent) with classification: `KEEP` / `REPLACE-WITH-GRIT` / `REPLACE-WITH-ICM` / `REPLACE-WITH-HELPER` / `ARCHIVE` / `DELETE`. The inventory pass covers `/Users/jasonlee/oyatie/**` (excluding `target/`, `node_modules/`, `.git/`) and the bominal surfaces the cutover touches (`/Users/jasonlee/bominal/agents/`, `/Users/jasonlee/bominal/docs/`).
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:5. **Parallel agent work is first-class.** The cutover and the subsequent `/ralplan`-driven implementation are expected to run multiple agents in parallel. Every agent claims its symbols with `grit claim --agent X --intent "…"` before any edit; `--with-deps` is used wherever the work crosses callee boundaries. `grit session` carves the multi-agent feature-branch space.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:6. **No git/gh shortcuts in CI either.** CI flows that previously invoked `gh pr create` or `git rebase --autosquash` for agent-authored work route through the same sanctioned helpers; the merge-gate hook validates that the PR's authoring sequence references a `grit done --agent` event rather than a manual rebase.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- Wrapping `gh` for *humans* on the terminal — RTK's `rtk gh`/`rtk git` savings are still fine for human terminal usage. The ban applies to agent execution paths.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- Re-implementing grit or icm primitives inside oyatie. We integrate the upstream tools; we do not fork them.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:2. **A2 — Inventory ledger committed.** `oyatie/docs/decisions/registry/placeholder-debt/adr-follow-ups.yaml#grit-cutover-inventory (superseded by ADR-0116)` (or equivalent) exists, classifies every file/dir/script under the inventory scope, and is referenced from `ADR-INDEX.md`. *Test*: ADR shape lane + every entry has a classification value from the closed set.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:3. **A3 — Orchestration glue archived.** `bominal/agents/ultragoal/{ledger.jsonl, goals.json, codex-goal-*.json, G004-reconciliation-blocker.md, PAUSE.md}` moved to `bominal/agents/ultragoal/archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/` then removed from the active path. `omx ultragoal checkpoint/complete-goals` flow is retired with a deprecation notice citing `grit done` + `icm store -t context-oyatie` as the successor. *Test*: `grit symbols` shows no active orchestration-glue paths; archive directory contains the moved set.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:5. **A5 — Agent-facing memory rewritten.** `oyatie/CLAUDE.md` and `oyatie/AGENTS.md` (and the `docs/AGENTS.md` they redirect to) remove every agent-instruction reference to `rtk git`, `rtk gh`, `git`, `gh`. The "sanctioned primitives" section names `grit`, `icm`, `oya-agent-read`. *Test*: a grep lane (`oya-governance-banned-primitives`) for the banned tokens in agent-instruction sections returns zero hits.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:6. **A6 — Hook + skill audit.** `agents/settings/claude.settings.json` and any project-level skill that previously called `git`/`gh` is rewritten to route through grit + icm + `oya-agent-read`. The existing `grit-claim-state-on-stop` Stop hook stays. *Test*: each touched file has a passing audit row in the inventory ledger.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:7. **A7 — Parallel-claim demo (session-less mode).** A reproducible demo proves N>1 agents can `grit claim` non-overlapping symbols, work in their auto-created `.grit/worktrees/<agent>/` directories in parallel, and land via `grit done --agent <agent>` without conflict. *Test*: a recorded session in `docs/runbooks/grit-parallel-claim-demo.md` shows the sequence with timestamps and `grit watch` event excerpts. Session-mode demo (`grit session start` + `grit session pr`) is scheduled-for-distinct-tracked-work until upstream session bug fix; tracked as a successor-IP runbook.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:9. **A9 — Spec-to-plan handoff path.** `/ralplan --consensus --direct .omc/scratch/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md` produces a plan at `.omc/plans/` that names this spec as Phase 0 input and routes execution to autopilot/team with parallel-grit-claim batches. *Test*: plan file exists and references this spec by path.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:1. **`grit` upstream is stable for the primitives we depend on** (`claim`, `worktree`, `done`, `session`, `assign`, `heartbeat`, `gc`, `watch`, `symbols`, `status`). Version 0.3.0 confirmed installed. If a primitive misbehaves, escalation is an upstream issue, not an oyatie patch.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:2. **`icm` upstream is stable** for `recall`, `recall-context`, `store -t -c -i -k`, `update`, `health`, `topics`. The user is already actively using icm (we stored 5 records this session).
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:7. **CI pipeline can be migrated incrementally** — agent flows route through grit before human flows are forced; human terminal usage of `rtk git`/`rtk gh` is unaffected.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:10. **The PR-merge flow under `grit done` produces something semantically equivalent to a GitHub PR.** If `grit done` only does local merge today, the helper layer extends to `oya-agent-write pr-finalize <session>` as a one-call atom that creates the PR record without exposing `gh` to agents.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  .grit/                           # grit local state (already exists)
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  agents/ultragoal/archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/   # archived glue (ledger.jsonl etc.)
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:1. grit claim --agent <id> --intent "<one-line>" <file::symbol>...
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:   # grit auto-creates a per-agent worktree at .grit/worktrees/<id>/
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:   # markdown/text files are NOT in the symbol index — claim them via file-path locks (future grit feature) or coordinate via icm
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:2. <agent works inside .grit/worktrees/<id>/>
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:3. agent reads via: grit symbols | grit status | grit watch | icm recall-context | oya-agent-read log/diff/pr-view
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:4. agent writes via: file edits inside its worktree (grit symbol-locks prevent cross-agent overwrite)
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:5. icm store -t {decisions-oyatie|context-oyatie|errors-resolved|preferences} per CLAUDE.md mandate
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:6. grit done --agent <id>        # rebase + merge worktree back to base + release locks in one atomic step
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:7. (optional, when grit session bug is fixed upstream) grit session pr   # creates PR for the session
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:**Known constraint — grit 0.3.0 session bug.** `grit session start` fails on this repo state with `git checkout -b failed: 'grit/<name>' is not a commit`. Root cause appears to be a missing default for the source-ref argument in grit's session-start codepath. Operating under **session-less mode** until upstream fix: agents claim symbols directly on `main` (or whatever the orchestrator's current base branch is), grit auto-worktrees per agent, `grit done` lands the worktree back to base. The `grit session pr` PR-creation primitive is **scheduled-for-distinct-tracked-work** until the upstream session bug ships a fix; in the interim, PR creation routes through `oya-agent-read`'s sibling write-helper or remains a human-orchestrator-only step. File the bug upstream at `rtk-ai/grit` as part of A6 deliverables.

docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| **icm (rtk-ai/icm)** | Upstream persistent-memory CLI. Topic-partitioned, importance-tagged, recall via keyword search. Survives session compaction. | External storage; project-canonical decisions duplicated into `oyatie/docs/` per Constraint 2. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| **oya-agent-read** | NEW thin sanctioned helper. Read-only wrapper over `git`/`gh` for operations grit doesn't cover. Audit-emitting. | `tools/oya-agent-read/` (to be created). |

docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| **Orchestration glue** | Pre-cutover artifacts (`ledger.jsonl`, `goals.json`, `codex-goal-*.json`, `omx ultragoal checkpoint`, `.codex/worktree_init.sh`, agent-facing RTK references) that duplicate grit/icm primitives. Deletion target. | `bominal/agents/ultragoal/archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/` after cutover |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| **Inventory ledger** | The ADR + tracked table that classifies every file/dir/script in scope before any deletion happens. | `oyatie/docs/decisions/registry/placeholder-debt/adr-follow-ups.yaml#grit-cutover-inventory (superseded by ADR-0116)` |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| **Parallel-claim demo** | A reproducible recording proving N>1 agents can work simultaneously on non-overlapping symbols and land via `grit done` without conflict. | `oyatie/docs/runbooks/grit-parallel-claim-demo.md` |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- Lane 3 (what is the direction shift): RESOLVED — agentic-pipeline mechanism, sanctioned primitives `{grit, icm, oya-agent-read}`, agent-side `git`/`gh` banned.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- `grit status` showed expired stale claims in bominal — exactly the failure mode `grit gc` handles natively, validating the "don't reinvent" thesis.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:1. (Initial goal): "/ralplan plan and implement oyatie as described. Use grit for full workflow from claim, work, done. /ultrawork or /batch when sensible. Begin with /deep-dive — major shift in direction after consolidation. I want a single source of truth."
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:3. "Because of grit, we no longer have to over engineer the agentic pipeline of foundry (merge conflict no longer possible as long as agents follow grit protocol)."
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:4. "We can implement what works from rtk-ai/grit and rtk-ai/icm to our pipeline."
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:5. "that means agents should not use git at all. or gh. everything is through grit pipeline."
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:7. (AskUserQuestion answers): PRD = oyatie/docs/PRD.md canonical; agent reads = icm + sanctioned helpers; ledger = all four open; inventory scope = "repo files directories scripts. source of truth and all files must live inside repo as tracked artifact."
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:The user owns two related repositories — `/Users/jasonlee/bominal/` (parent monorepo with extensive planning corpus) and `/Users/jasonlee/oyatie/` (child product/codebase). They want a single source of truth (SoT) for `oyatie` before applying a major direction shift, then to hand off to `/ralplan` + grit `claim → work → done`. Four doc paths were investigated: `bominal/agents/ultragoal/`, `bominal/docs/`, `oyatie/.omx/`, `oyatie/docs/`.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:2. *"Because of grit, we no longer have to over engineer the agentic pipeline of foundry (merge conflict no longer possible as long as agents follow grit protocol)."*
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:3. *"We can implement what works from https://github.com/rtk-ai/grit and https://github.com/rtk-ai/icm to our pipeline."*
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:4. *"agents should not use git at all. or gh. everything is through grit pipeline."*
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:The direction shift is therefore not a product re-scoping; it is an **agentic-pipeline simplification + sanctioned-primitive lockdown**: grit becomes the only agent-side coordination/state-transition primitive, icm becomes the only cross-session knowledge primitive, and direct `git`/`gh` invocation by agents is banned.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| 1 | **SoT-ownership / orchestration** (Lane 2) is the structural problem | High | Strong | All 4 corpora declare authority but the bominal-parent → oyatie-child boundary is implicit; canonical-home symlink rule is declared in `ultragoal/2026-05-12-foundry-ultragoal-mega-plan.md §0.2` but unenforced; orchestration glue (ledger.jsonl, codex-goal-*.json, omx ultragoal checkpoint) duplicates what grit's primitives already provide |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- Orchestration glue duplicates grit primitives. `bominal/agents/ultragoal/` carries 14+ artifacts that grit + icm subsume:
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  - `goals.json` + 9× `codex-goal-*.json` → `grit claim --agent X --intent "…"` + `icm store -t goals-oyatie`
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  - `ledger.jsonl` → `grit watch` event stream (room.sock) + grit lock state
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  - `omx ultragoal checkpoint` / `complete-goals` → `grit done --agent X` + `icm store -t context-oyatie`

docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:  - `.codex/worktree_init.sh` → `grit worktree` primitive
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- Inspection of seven suspect `oya-foundry-*-kernel` crates (`claim-ceiling`, `authority-cohesion`, `bypass`, `pr-traceability`, `pre-push`, `quality-lane`, `cohesion-fitness`) shows they are **product-quality fitness/policy kernels**, not agent-coordination kernels — they survive the grit pivot. The "over-engineered" surface is the orchestration glue layer in `bominal/agents/` and the codex/omx wrappers, not the in-tree foundry crates.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:Per the deep-dive skill, every MOVE candidate must be classified by `ownership_scope` with cross-boundary flags. Lane 2 surfaced 7 cross-boundary candidates; this synthesis enriches them with the grit-pivot lens (what the candidate becomes under the simplification).

Under the grit pivot this doc becomes ground-zero for the new agentic-pipeline spec — promote in oyatie, do not move to bominal. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| `oyatie/.omx/ultragoal/` | symlink to `bominal/agents/ultragoal/` per §0.2 canonical-home rule | personal-config (session state) → shared-config (canonical planning) | cross-boundary | **no** | Session-scoped working state. Under the grit pivot the right answer is DELETE: grit's worktree + lock + done lifecycle replaces `.omx/ultragoal/` session state. Do not symlink; retire. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| `bominal/agents/ultragoal/{ledger.jsonl, goals.json, codex-goal-*.json, G004-reconciliation-blocker.md, PAUSE.md}` | archive then delete | shared-config (active orchestration) → archive | same-scope (within bominal) | yes (archive default; delete after grit cutover verified) | These are the orchestration-glue deletion targets. Archive under `bominal/agents/ultragoal/archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/` so history is preserved, then delete from active path. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| `oyatie/.codex/worktree_init.sh` (if exists) and any equivalent codex/gemini per-agent init scripts | delete | personal-config (per-agent init) → removed | same-scope | yes | Replaced wholesale by `grit worktree` + `grit claim`. Inventory pass must enumerate every such script before deletion. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:| `oyatie/CLAUDE.md` RTK section + `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` RTK section (as they apply to agent instructions) | rewrite to remove agent-side `rtk git`/`rtk gh` references | personal-config / shared-config → updated | **flagged for user decision** | RTK is the user's personal token-optimization layer. Banning agent-side `rtk git` is project-level; banning all `rtk git` in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` would extend the rule to every project the user owns. Default: edit oyatie/CLAUDE.md only; leave global RTK as-is unless user explicitly broadens the rule. |
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- **Counter**: The direction shift the user disclosed is *agentic-pipeline simplification*, not a content reframe. Under that shift, the orchestration glue (Hypothesis 1 evidence) is the deletion target — `ledger.jsonl`, `codex-goal-*.json`, omx ultragoal checkpoint flow, `.codex/worktree_init.sh`. The contradiction ledger (Hypothesis 2 evidence) is *preserved* because contradictions are product-quality concerns, which survive the grit pivot. Therefore Hypothesis 1 leads under the disclosed direction shift; Hypothesis 2 is real but orthogonal.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:The "no single source of truth for oyatie" pain is **structurally an ownership/orchestration boundary problem (Hypothesis 1)** layered on a real-but-tracked contradiction backlog (Hypothesis 2). The major direction shift the user is about to apply does not touch product content — it absorbs the agentic-orchestration layer into upstream tools (`rtk-ai/grit` for coordination, `rtk-ai/icm` for cross-session knowledge). Under the shift, much of the duplicated authority surface (oyatie's `.omx/ultragoal/`, bominal's `agents/ultragoal/` orchestration glue, codex per-agent init scripts) becomes deletion target; the in-tree foundry fitness/policy kernels survive because they govern product quality, not agent coordination.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:2. **Agentic-pipeline layer** (Hypothesis 1 under the direction shift): one canonical operating contract that names grit + icm as the only sanctioned primitives, bans agent-side git/gh, lists every deletion target with an archive→retire plan, and rewrites the oyatie `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` to reflect the new rule.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- `grit symbols` enumerates code symbols; can it answer "what files changed in the last claim" the way `git diff` does?
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- `grit show-session` / `grit watch` exist but their read-path semantics for "give me the state of N concurrent agents right now" are not yet specified.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- For history operations agents currently use `git log` for (e.g., a debugger agent reading the last 20 commits to find a regression), grit has no equivalent — does the answer come from icm `recall -t commits-oyatie`, or does the agent invoke a *non-agent* helper that wraps `git log` and returns the result?
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:- For PR-equivalent operations agents currently use `gh pr create` for, `grit done` is the closest primitive — does `grit done` create a PR on a remote, or does it merge locally and leave PR creation to a separate non-agent flow?
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:1. **Read `grit done --help`, `grit watch --help`, `grit symbols --help`, `grit session --help`, `grit show-session --help` (if present), and `grit assign --help` in full to map the agent read-side surface.** Document which `git`/`gh` read-side calls are absorbed natively vs which require a non-agent helper wrapper.
docs/decisions/specs/deep-dive-trace-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md:   - "For agent read-side surface where `grit` has no equivalent (e.g., `git log`-style commit-history queries), is the answer: (a) icm `recall -t commits-oyatie`, (b) a sanctioned non-agent helper, or (c) extend grit with the missing read primitive?"
docs/decisions/ADR-0097-foundry-account-adapter-rename-target-slot-last.md:This ADR records the rename decision. **The actual crate rename is a prerequisite task separate from M02-P06 implementation** and executes before any Wave 3 grit units open against these crates.
docs/decisions/ADR-0097-foundry-account-adapter-rename-target-slot-last.md:- Wave 3 grit units can open with conformant crate names on day-1.
docs/decisions/ADR-0097-foundry-account-adapter-rename-target-slot-last.md:- The rename itself must precede Wave 3a grit claim (`grit claim --agent worker-3a …`) or
docs/decisions/ADR-0097-foundry-account-adapter-rename-target-slot-last.md:1. **Prerequisite task** — Execute the three-crate rename before Wave 3a grit unit opens.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:  {grit, icm, oya-tooling-agent-read}. Direct git/gh permitted only with
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:# ADR-0053: grit + icm + oya-tooling-agent-read as sole sanctioned primitives for agentic work
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Siblings landing in parallel:** ADR-0052 (pre-grit artifact inventory), ADR-0054 (grit scaffold-claim pattern)
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:The set of agent-callable coordination and state-transition primitives is fixed at exactly three: `grit` (upstream `rtk-ai/grit`), `icm` (upstream `rtk-ai/icm`), and `oya-tooling-agent-read` (a thin, read-only, audit-emitting helper shipped at `tools/oya-tooling-agent-read/`). No agent execution path may invoke `git` or `gh` directly. Adding to the sanctioned set requires a successor-IP ADR with explicit justification of why neither `grit` nor `icm` already covers the operation.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:The sanctioned-primitive set above is the **steady-state contract**, valid from the moment the banned-primitives fitness lane activates at the P5 merge boundary. During the **cutover bootstrap window** — phases P0.5 (scaffold-claim ADR), P1 (inventory ADR), P2 (ship `oya-tooling-agent-read`) — the helper does not yet exist, so the temporarily-effective sanctioned set is `{grit, icm}` plus an explicit carve-out for read-only `git`/`gh` invocations strictly to inspect prior helper scaffolds, prior tooling crates, and existing repo state. Every such bootstrap-window invocation is recorded in the inventory ledger as a one-time bootstrap row (column: `bootstrap_window: true`, `invocation: <command>`, `purpose: <one-line>`, `agent_or_human: <id>`). The banned-primitives fitness lane (`oya-governance-banned-primitives`) is **defined** in P4 (rewrite agent memory) but **activated** at P5 merge — between P4 and P5 the lane exists, the rewrite exists, but enforcement is off. The lane goes live with the P5 merge commit; from that commit forward, the carve-out is closed.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Eliminate merge-conflict-as-failure-mode**: `grit` symbol-locking guarantees that parallel agents cannot stomp each other's work, removing the entire class of "two agents touched the same function" defects.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Eliminate orchestration-glue rot**: every pre-cutover artifact at `bominal/agents/ultragoal/{ledger.jsonl, goals.json, codex-goal-*.json, G004-reconciliation-blocker.md, PAUSE.md}` and `omx ultragoal checkpoint/complete-goals` exists to solve coordination problems `grit` solves natively. Deleting the glue removes ~12 artifacts of bespoke state-tracking that drifts from reality (LEDG-008-style premise mismatches, MFL-0010-style stub residue).
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Cross-session knowledge persistence is a separate concern**: `icm` already solves it. Building a second memory layer in-tree (project-memory.json, notepad.md as authority, etc.) duplicates a working upstream tool.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Linus-style discipline**: reshape the data structures so special cases disappear, rather than layering shims. The "agent goal mismatch detection" subroutine in `omx ultragoal checkpoint` is a special case that exists only because the goal state was held in a separate file from the claim state. Under `grit`, claim = intent, so the special case vanishes.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Bidirectional audit traceability**: every `grit claim` is an event; every `icm store` is an event; every `oya-tooling-agent-read` invocation emits to the audit chain. Direct `git`/`gh` from agents leaves no equivalent trail — the rule closes that observability gap.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:The named individual(s) authorized in `oyatie/docs/RACI-OWNERSHIP.md` (row `human-orchestrator-cutover` added as part of P0.5) to perform the three carve-out invocations (`git mv`, `git rm`, `gh issue create`) during cutover phases P6, P7, and P9. Every carve-out invocation by a human orchestrator is recorded via `icm store -t cutover-orchestrator-actions -c '<action>' -i critical` **BEFORE** execution; the icm row is the audit trail. Orchestrator authorization does not grant general `git`/`gh` privileges — it is scoped to the named cutover phases and their specific commands. Steady-state agent operations never invoke this carve-out.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:Keep `omx ultragoal checkpoint`, `codex-goal-*.json`, `ledger.jsonl`, `G004-reconciliation-blocker.md`, etc. Layer agent skills directly on `git`/`gh`. **Rejected because**: the orchestration glue is bespoke, untested at the data-model layer (the `G004-reconciliation-blocker.md` artifact is literal evidence of a state-machine bug — `omx` couldn't reconcile a paused vs. unknown goal state, so the agent halted), and every agent-side `git checkout`/`git rebase`/`gh pr create` is an opportunity for divergent local convention. The user's directive ("agents should not use git at all. or gh. everything is through grit pipeline") closes this alternative explicitly.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:### Alternative B — grit + icm only; no helper CLI
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:Ban `git`/`gh` and refuse to provide any escape hatch. Agents have only what `grit` and `icm` natively expose. **Rejected because**: some agent workflows genuinely need read-side operations grit does not cover today (e.g., debugger reading the last 20 commits to find a regression, code-reviewer reading PR comment threads). Refusing the escape hatch either blocks those workflows or pushes agents toward shadow-tool invocation (e.g., `bash -c "git log..."` smuggled through), which is worse than a sanctioned, audited helper. The helper is small, read-only, and audit-emitting — Linus-acceptable, not bureaucracy.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:Fork or rewrite the coordination layer inside the oyatie repo (e.g., `crates/oya-foundry-agent-coordinator-kernel/`). **Rejected because**: it duplicates `grit`. The user explicitly noted "we can implement what works from rtk-ai/grit and rtk-ai/icm to our pipeline" — the recommendation is integration, not fork. A custom in-tree coordinator would also need its own maintenance, fitness lanes, and ADRs — exactly the over-engineering this direction shift removes.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- Reading `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` no longer ambiguously prescribes `rtk git` to agents (it stays for human terminal use); agent CLAUDE.md sections are scrubbed.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- Upstream dependency on `rtk-ai/grit` 0.3.0+. The `grit session start` bug (documented in `.omc/scratch/pre-cutover-drafts-2026-05-12.md §Draft 1`) is a real blocker for the `grit session pr` flow until fixed upstream. Workaround is **session-less mode** documented in the spec.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- New-crate creation has a chicken-and-egg with grit symbol-locking (you cannot claim a symbol that doesn't exist yet). Resolved by the **scaffold-claim pattern** in `ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md` (lifts from `.omc/scratch/pre-cutover-drafts-2026-05-12.md §Draft 2`).
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- Agents lose the convenience of `rtk gh pr view` etc.; they must route through `oya-tooling-agent-read pr-view`, which is one indirection.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- The `RTK` instructions in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` (user-machine global) are unchanged. Human terminal usage of `rtk git`/`rtk gh` is unaffected.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **The cutover itself runs under a one-time human-orchestrator carve-out** for `git mv` (P6 archive), `git rm` (P7 delete), and `gh issue create` (P9 upstream-bug filing). Post-cutover, the agent lifecycle is `grit claim → work → grit done` for every operation; the cutover commits themselves are not retroactively flowed through `grit done`. A future architect reading the cutover commits will see no `grit done --agent` references for those specific landing commits — this is intentional, by-design, and documented here so the absence is not misread as a broken model.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:1. **File upstream grit bug** for `grit session start` at `rtk-ai/grit` (draft at `.omc/scratch/pre-cutover-drafts-2026-05-12.md §Draft 1`). Track in oyatie's `RISK-REGISTER.md` under a new row.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:2. **ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md** for the new-crate chicken-and-egg resolution. Draft at `.omc/scratch/pre-cutover-drafts-2026-05-12.md §Draft 2`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:4. **Add inventory-tracker fitness lane** for the `archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/` directory — once a file is archived, the active-path equivalent must NOT exist (and vice versa for KEEP-class files).
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:5. **Schedule re-evaluation of the helper surface in 90 days** (2026-08-12) — if `grit` upstream adds `grit log`/`grit pr-view`/`grit diff` natively, deprecate the corresponding `oya-tooling-agent-read` subcommands and shrink the helper.
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **ADR path:** `docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md`
docs/decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md:- **Sibling ADRs landing in parallel:** ADR-0052 (pre-grit artifact inventory), ADR-0054 (grit scaffold-claim pattern)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:# ADR-0116: Retire external agent-coordination tooling (grit, rtk, icm, vox) in favour of the Foundry pipeline
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:> **Supersedes:** ADR-0054 (grit scaffold-claim pattern)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:> **purpose:** Formally retire grit, rtk, icm, and vox from the repo's prescribed agent-coordination surface. The in-repo Foundry pipeline (M01-P18 substrate landed in PR #5 + the wave-A webhook-receiver work) is now the sole canonical workflow for concurrent agent work.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:Prior ADRs (ADR-0052, ADR-0053, ADR-0054) elevated `grit` and `icm` as "sanctioned coordination primitives" for new-crate scaffolds and concurrent agent work. `rtk` was used as a cargo shim for token-cost accounting, and `vox` was inventoried as an additional coordination surface. Each of these is an out-of-repo tool maintained outside the oyatie tree.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:1. **Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) is live in-repo.** PR #5 landed ADR-0110/0111/0112/0113, which together define the end-to-end agentic VCS substrate: webhook intake, router, admission-gate, projected-merge-state, conflict-kernel, changeset-state, review-mergequeue-kernel. Wave-A (webhook-receiver intake + routing + projected-merge-state crates) is merged. The Foundry pipeline is the in-repo replacement for everything `grit` and `icm` previously coordinated outside the repo.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:3. **No-out-of-repo-coordination invariant.** The long-term operating contract (sunset note already present in `CLAUDE.md`) said grit/icm/rtk/vox would all leave the repo once Oya VCS and Foundry went live. Both conditions are now met.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- `grit` (claim/work/done, scaffold-locks)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- `icm` (coordination-lock topics, scaffold-locks-oyatie fallback)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- `rtk` (cargo shim and command rewrites)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- `vox` (inventoried but unused)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| `grit claim/work/done` | **Foundry pipeline** (M01-P18): per-agent worktree (Layer 0 isolation) → PR off `dev` → webhook-receiver → router → admission-gate + merge-queue (ADR-0111 projected-merge-state + conflict-kernel) → auto-merge on review+CI green | ADR-0110/0111/0112/0113 + PR #5 + wave-A webhook-receiver |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| `icm scaffold-locks` (topic `scaffold-locks-oyatie`) | **Foundry pipeline** Layer-0 isolation (per-agent worktree). Concurrent-safe-paths registry handles file-scope coordination at admission-gate time; no shared mutable file scope exists once each agent works in its own worktree | ADR-0111 §"Conflict-avoidance pre-admit gate" + `registry/vcs/concurrent-safe-paths.yaml` |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| `rtk` cargo shim | Direct cargo via `~/.rustup/toolchains/<channel>/bin/cargo`. No Foundry-pipeline involvement; this is a local build-tool concern only | (no replacement needed) |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| `vox` | No active use — formally retired without replacement | (no replacement needed) |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| ADR deprecation | `docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md` | Status → `Deprecated 2026-05-16`; added "Superseded by ADR-0116" line at top; content otherwise preserved |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:| Hook removal | `.omc/hooks/grit-claim-intent-gate.sh` | Removed — the gate guards `grit claim` invocations that no longer occur |
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:The repo-wide scrub of historical references to grit/rtk/icm/vox is **out of scope** for this PR: ADRs ADR-0052/0053/0054 and prior runbooks/plans/specs/evidence files reference these tools as historical record. Per the brief, history is preserved; only **prescribing** files (CLAUDE.md fenced agent surface + the sanctioned-primitives runbook + the active grit hook) are rewritten. Surviving mentions are enumerated under "Verification" below.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- Historical references to grit/icm/rtk/vox remain in ADRs, plans, evidence files, and registries. These are knowingly preserved as history; a future cleanup pass MAY rewrite parenthetical references but must not delete the historical record.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- The `Bash(grit *)` permission entry that may exist in agent settings is left in place under deny-by-omission (commands will not be issued; the permission is inert).
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- Agents MUST NOT invoke `grit`, `rtk`, `icm`, or `vox` for any new work as of 2026-05-16.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- Direct cargo invocations bypass the (now-retired) rtk shim: `~/.rustup/toolchains/1.95.0-aarch64-apple-darwin/bin/cargo …`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- **Keep grit as transitional indefinitely.** Rejected — leaves two coordination surfaces in the prescribed agent contract, doubles the cognitive load, and contradicts the long-standing sunset note in CLAUDE.md that promised both surfaces would not coexist past Foundry/Oya VCS go-live.
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- ADR-0054 — grit scaffold-claim pattern (now Deprecated 2026-05-16; superseded by this ADR)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- ADR-0052 — grit/icm tooling inventory (historical)
docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md:- ADR-0053 — grit + icm as sanctioned coordination primitives (historical)
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:purpose: Retire the one-time archive-orphan fitness lane and pre-grit archive payload after ADR-0116 establishes the Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) as the canonical VCS substrate.
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:M01-P08-IP-008 created a temporary `archive-orphan` lane to verify that pre-cutover Bominal ultragoal state moved under `bominal/agents/ultragoal/archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/` and that active originals disappeared. That gate was useful for the grit-era cutover boundary, but it is now a stale one-time cleanup lane.
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:ADR-0116 retires grit, rtk, icm, and vox from the prescribed agent-coordination surface. The Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) is now the canonical VCS substrate: isolated worktree branch, PR against `dev`, webhook receiver, router, admission gate, projected merge state, conflict kernel, merge queue, reviewer approval, and CI green before merge.
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:1. It preserves a deleted pre-grit archive payload as if it were still operationally useful.
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:2. It makes an old grit-era invariant look like an active M01-P18 admission primitive.

docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:- The repo no longer carries a one-time pre-grit archive payload.
docs/decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md:**ADR-0108 lifecycle-policy waiver:** PR #13 bypassed the canonical sunset → deprecation → removal window (ADR-0108 default 30d + 90d). The waiver was implicit ("one-time pre-grit cutover hygiene with payload deleted in same ChangeSet — sunset window cannot produce new violations when inputs are deleted simultaneously"). Codifying that exception as a `one_time_lane: true` carve-out in ADR-0108 is filed as `F-ADR0108-ONETIME-LANE-CARVEOUT` (see registries/cross-cutting/fixuptasks.jsonl).

This content is in code + telemetry, not docs.

docs/decisions/ADR-0108-sunset-lifecycle-automation.md:  - ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:# ADR-0054: Resolve new-crate chicken-and-egg via grit scaffold-claim pattern (icm-coordination-lock fallback)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:> **Superseded by ADR-0116 (2026-05-16)** — external agent-coordination tooling (grit, rtk, icm, vox) is retired; the Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) is the canonical workflow. See `docs/decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:> **purpose:** Resolve the new-crate chicken-and-egg with grit symbol-locking via the icm-coordination-lock fallback pattern (scaffold-locks-oyatie topic). Cargo.toml::workspace_members is verified NOT indexed by grit at v0.3.0, so the primary scaffold-claim path is the icm fallback.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:> **Related:** ADR-0052 (grit/icm tooling inventory), ADR-0053 (grit + icm as sanctioned coordination primitives), ADR-0015 (flat-crates layout), ADR-0041 (GitOps trunk-based development)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:Deprecated 2026-05-16 — superseded by ADR-0116. The Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) replaces the grit/icm scaffold-claim pattern in its entirety; new-crate scaffolds now use plain `git mv` inside a per-agent `git worktree`, with the resulting PR entering the Foundry pipeline (admission-gate → merge-queue → auto-merge on review+CI green).
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:Original status (historical): Accepted — 2026-05-12. The icm-coordination-lock fallback was the canonical scaffold-claim path at grit v0.3.0. The `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` primary path was blocked pending a grit upstream fix (see Follow-ups §1). Both of these are now retired per ADR-0116.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:`grit claim` requires a real indexed code symbol in the form `<file>::<Identifier>`. A *new* crate (e.g., `tools/oya-tooling-agent-read/`) has no source files yet, hence no indexed symbols, hence cannot be locked via `grit claim` before scaffolding begins. The cutover plan creates new crates — this is a concrete gap in the spec.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The proposed resolution is a **scaffold-claim pattern**: lock a workspace-level coordination point (`Cargo.toml::workspace_members`) for the duration of crate creation, then re-index so normal symbol-level claims can proceed. However, Lane 3 of the deep-dive trace (run 2026-05-12) confirmed via `grit symbols` that `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` returns **zero matches** — the field is not indexed by grit at v0.3.0. File-level path locks are also not supported in grit v0.3.0. The primary scaffold-claim path is therefore unavailable today.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:This ADR formalises the **icm-coordination-lock fallback** as the canonical scaffold-claim path and records the conditions under which the primary path (`Cargo.toml::workspace_members` grit claim) supersedes it once grit gains the required indexing capability.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:Sibling ADRs: ADR-0052 inventories the grit/icm tooling surface; ADR-0053 establishes grit + icm as the sanctioned coordination primitives replacing the retired omx/ultragoal stack.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The canonical scaffold-claim path for new-crate creation is the **icm-coordination-lock fallback**:
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:1. Before scaffolding any new crate, the authoring agent opens a scaffold window in the `scaffold-locks-oyatie` icm topic.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:3. The authoring agent closes the window after scaffolding completes and `grit init` has been re-run.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:This is slower than a native grit lock but correct. It is the **only valid path** at grit v0.3.0.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` primary path is **reserved for future adoption** once grit upstream indexes the field (see Follow-ups §1). When that condition is met, an amending note to this ADR records the promotion; the icm fallback is then demoted to secondary-only.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- **Correctness over speed.** A silent race between two agents scaffolding into the same workspace directory corrupts `Cargo.toml` and blocks CI. An explicit coordination lock, even via icm, eliminates the race.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- **Minimal new mechanism.** icm is already a sanctioned primitive (ADR-0053). No new tooling is introduced.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- **Forward compatibility.** The pattern is defined so that the primary path (`Cargo.toml::workspace_members` grit claim) can slot in without changing the sequence shape — only step 1 and step 4 change.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- **Lane 3 verification.** The grit non-indexing of `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` is an empirically confirmed fact (zero matches, 2026-05-12), not an assumption. The decision is grounded in observed behaviour, not speculation.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:### Alternative A — `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` grit claim (primary path, rejected for now)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Proposal:** Claim `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` via `grit claim --agent <id> --intent "scaffold ..." Cargo.toml::workspace_members` to take a native grit lock on the workspace manifest for the scaffolding window.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Pros:** Native grit lock; visible in `grit watch`; automatically released by `grit done`; no icm dependency for this step.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Cons:** **Blocked.** Lane 3 of the deep-dive trace confirmed `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` returns zero matches in `grit symbols` at v0.3.0. Attempting the claim produces a raw sqlite FK violation (symbol-not-found surfaced as FK error — documented in the upstream bug report). The path is unavailable until grit upstream adds indexing for Cargo.toml fields.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:### Alternative B — Per-file-path lock via grit (rejected — not supported)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Proposal:** Use a hypothetical `grit claim --path Cargo.toml` or `grit lock --file Cargo.toml` to lock at the file level rather than the symbol level.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Pros:** Would not require grit to index TOML fields, only to support a file-level lock primitive.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Cons:** **Not supported.** grit v0.3.0 does not expose a file-level lock primitive. All locks are symbol-scoped (`<file>::<Identifier>`). This is a missing feature, not a configuration gap. Filed as a successor-IP upstream request (Follow-ups §2).
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:**Verdict:** Rejected. Does not exist in the current grit surface.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The icm fallback is the only mechanism that is both (a) available today and (b) correct under concurrent agent execution. It re-uses an already-sanctioned primitive (ADR-0053), introduces no new dependencies, and is forward-compatible with native grit promotion once the upstream gap is closed.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- Pattern is auditable: `icm recall -t scaffold-locks-oyatie` shows all open and closed scaffold windows.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- icm is a slower and less integrated lock than a native grit symbol lock. It relies on agents actively polling `icm recall`; there is no TTL-based auto-release or `grit watch` event for icm lock state.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- The window-open/window-closed discipline is manual. An agent crash or interrupted session can leave a window permanently open. Recovery requires a human `icm update` or a monitoring script.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- Any agent intending to scaffold a new crate **must** run `icm recall -t scaffold-locks-oyatie` first and back off if any window is open for an overlapping path.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- The `oya-governance-scaffold-claim-pattern` fitness lane enforces that no new crate directory appears in git history without a corresponding icm scaffold-lock open/close pair in `scaffold-locks-oyatie`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- If a stale open window is found, the on-call agent inspects icm for the originating agent id and either confirms the window is still live or closes it manually.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The following is a complete scaffold-claim sequence. The `icm store` calls are mandatory; omitting them violates the fitness lane.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:icm store \
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:1. icm store -t scaffold-locks-oyatie \
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:4. grit done --agent <id>          # lands the scaffold to base
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:5. grit init                       # re-index so the new crate's symbols are claimable
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:6. icm store -t scaffold-locks-oyatie \
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:7. <subsequent agents use normal grit claim against the new crate's symbols>
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:icm store \
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:After step 7, subsequent agents may claim symbols inside `tools/oya-tooling-agent-read/` via standard `grit claim --agent <id> --intent "..." "tools/oya-tooling-agent-read/src/lib.rs::<Symbol>"`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:1. **File upstream grit issue — `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` not indexed.** The `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` symbol returns zero results in `grit symbols` at v0.3.0. Upstream fix needed: index Cargo manifest fields (at minimum `workspace.members`, `package.name`) so that workspace-level scaffold locks can be taken natively. When this lands, amend this ADR to promote the primary path and demote the icm fallback. Ticket: file at `rtk-ai/grit` (Draft 1 of `.omc/scratch/pre-cutover-drafts-2026-05-12.md` documents the FK-violation symptom and suggested upstream fix).
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:2. **File upstream grit issue — file-level lock primitive.** Even without TOML field indexing, a `grit claim --path <file>` or `grit lock --file <file>` primitive would enable file-level locks as a scaffold coordination point. File as a separate upstream feature request at `rtk-ai/grit`. Revisit when grit ships a workspace-level lock primitive.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:3. **icm stale-window recovery runbook.** Author a short runbook at `docs/runbooks/icm-scaffold-lock-recovery.md` covering: how to detect a stale open window, how to confirm the originating agent is no longer active, and how to close the window manually.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- Lane 3 deep-dive trace, 2026-05-12 — verification that `Cargo.toml::workspace_members` returns zero matches in `grit symbols` at v0.3.0

docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- ADR-0053 — grit + icm as sanctioned coordination primitives (sibling)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:- ADR-0041 — GitOps trunk-based development (scaffold lands to base via `grit done`)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:creation: no source files → no indexed symbols → cannot take a native grit lock.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:`crates/oya-tenancy-kernel/src/lib.rs::TenantId`) is valid for a grit
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:at grit v0.3.0.
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:The icm-coordination-lock fallback generalises naturally: a rename window is
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:1. icm store -t scaffold-locks-oyatie \
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:5. grit done --agent <id>          (lands the rename batch to base)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:6. grit init                       (re-indexes new symbol paths)
docs/decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md:7. icm store -t scaffold-locks-oyatie \

docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:  Canonical inventory ledger for the grit/icm cutover; classifies every file/dir/script under oyatie/ and bominal/agents/ + bominal/docs/ scope by closed-set action (KEEP/KEEP+ANNOTATE/REPLACE-WITH-GRIT/REPLACE-WITH-ICM/REPLACE-WITH-HELPER/ARCHIVE/DELETE/FLAG-FOR-USER).

docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:# ADR-0052: Canonical Inventory Ledger for the grit/icm Cutover
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:The grit/icm cutover (per `.omc/plans/ralplan-oyatie-sst-consolidation.md`, Option A — strict-phased, archive-first) requires that **no artifact leaves the active path without a committed ADR row classifying it**. This is §Constraints item 3 of the plan, stated as: "Inventory precedes deletion."
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:Before this ADR, no single authoritative ledger classified all 223 artifact rows now in scope. The `ultragoal/` orchestration glue (`ledger.jsonl`, `goals.json`, `codex-goal-*.json`, `G004-reconciliation-blocker.md`, `PAUSE.md`) was treated as live coordination state even though its semantic content is wholly absorbed by `grit claim`/`grit done`/`grit watch` state and `icm store -t goals-oyatie`. The absence of a committed classification ledger created two risks:
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:**Alt 2 — A standalone JSON ledger at `docs/machine-readable/inventory-grit-cutover.json`.**
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:- **(b) Master Plan principles** — P1 (inventory precedes deletion), P3 (authoritative = repo-tracked), P7 (reshape data to eliminate special cases: `G004-reconciliation-blocker.md` and `PAUSE.md` disappear because grit's data model has no place for them, not because we add a shim).
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:Sibling ADRs ADR-0053 (sanctioned primitives closed set) and ADR-0054 (grit scaffold-claim pattern) land in the same wave and are cross-cited here because their acceptance is a precondition for the REPLACE-WITH-GRIT and REPLACE-WITH-ICM action classes to have executable meaning.

docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:| bominal/agents/settings/ | dir | KEEP | null | A8 | Agent settings; A6 audit required (any git/gh calls must route through grit+icm+oya-tooling-agent-read) |
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:| oyatie/CLAUDE.md RTK section | Remove agent-side `rtk git`/`rtk gh` references | shared-config → updated | same-scope | KEEP+ANNOTATE oyatie/CLAUDE.md; add sanctioned-primitives section | Agent ban is project-level; global ~/ RTK is user's personal token optimization. User must decide whether to extend ban to global config. **Default: edit oyatie/ only; flag global RTK for user decision.** |
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:| (none identified) | - | - | All authoritative state appears tracked or properly ephemeral. .gitignored dirs (`.grit/`, `.omx/`, `.omc/state/`) are session-scoped ephemera or external tool state, not canonical authority. Per Constraint 2, all canonical authority is repo-tracked. |
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:2. **ADR-0054 — grit scaffold-claim pattern.** Owner: `foundry`. Lands in the same wave (parallel). Defines the `icm-coordination-lock` fallback for new-crate phases where `grit symbols` cannot index `Cargo.toml::workspace_members`.
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:5. **P6 — Archive moves.** Owner: human orchestrator + `foundry`. Move all 15 `ARCHIVE`-class rows from `bominal/agents/ultragoal/` to `archive/pre-grit-cutover-2026-05-12/`. Gate: this ADR merged + `oya-governance-archive-orphan` lane scaffolded.
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:7. **FLAG-FOR-USER — Global RTK extension.** Owner: human principal. Decide whether to extend the agent-instruction grit/icm primitive ban to `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`. Default per spec §Non-Goals: no; scope is `oyatie/` only until the user explicitly broadens it.
docs/decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md:- ADR-0054: grit scaffold-claim pattern (sibling, parallel wave) — icm-coordination-lock fallback for new-crate phases
docs/decisions/ADR-0069-active-machine-readable-artifact-contract.md:3. **Validation** — Pre-commit / pre-push / pre-grit-claim / LSP hook for write-time validation.
docs/decisions/ADR-0069-active-machine-readable-artifact-contract.md:6. **Selfupdate** — Trigger-driven incremental drift correction (post-grit-done-hook, ci-lane-on-trigger, scheduled-job).
docs/decisions/ADR-0069-active-machine-readable-artifact-contract.md:9. **Provenance** — Author + reviewer + autogenerated_by + last_modified_at chain, with SLSA-L3 target and grit-done-seal interim.
docs/decisions/ADR-0069-active-machine-readable-artifact-contract.md:The current commit landing this ADR will use `rtk git commit` as documented historical violation per the same protocol gap that was logged for commit `5880ce0`. Subsequent state transitions on the new validator crate use `grit claim --agent <id> --intent <slice>` → work → `grit done`. Scaffold-locks-oyatie ICM fallback for symbol-less doc-only edits.

docs/architecture/standards-corpus-line-audit-2026-05-21.md:### 3.3 Retired external-agent-tooling drift — grit / icm / rtk / vox per ADR-0116
docs/architecture/standards-corpus-line-audit-2026-05-21.md:**Canonical directive:** ADR-0116 (2026-05-16) retired grit, icm, rtk, and vox as external agent-coordination tooling. All references to these tools in standards must be removed or replaced with plain `git` / `gh` / `cargo` equivalents.

Including them in `related_adrs` without a supersession annotation implies they are still active authorities. Every standard that lists them must either remove them or annotate them as "superseded by ADR-0116".

docs/AGENTS.md:Agent-callable coordination and state-transition primitives are a closed set: Oya VCS compatibility ratchet claim / verify / done / promote plus the `oya-vcs-admission` CI lane, all delivered through the Foundry pipeline (M01-P18). Oya VCS owns claims, ChangeBundles, review/fix, controller rebase, merge queue, promotion, and lock release until the task #38 `oya git` drop-in surface lands with explicit policy verbs split out. ADR-0052/ADR-0053/ADR-0054 are historical records only; the external coordination tools they sanctioned (grit, icm, rtk, vox) are retired per ADR-0116 (2026-05-16).
docs/AGENTS.md:External agent-coordination tooling (grit, rtk, icm, vox) is retired per ADR-0116. New crate scaffolds use plain `git mv` inside a per-agent `git worktree`; concurrent-file coordination happens at admission-gate time via `registry/vcs/concurrent-safe-paths.yaml` (ADR-0111 conflict-avoidance pre-admit gate). No external coordination tool participates in the prescribed surface.
docs/AGENTS.md:retirement_note: legacy grit/icm/rtk/vox are retired per ADR-0116; omx/omc/oya-tooling-agent-read remain compatibility/provenance-only during the cutover window. `oya git <git-subcommand>` is the git drop-in surface; Oya VCS remains the compatibility policy-ratchet spelling for claim / verify / done / promote until those policy verbs split explicitly.
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0052 | 2026-05-12 | Superseded | Canonical inventory ledger for the grit/icm cutover | Canonical inventory ledger for the grit/icm cutover; classifies every file/dir/script under oyatie/ and bominal/agents/ + bominal/docs/ scope by closed-set action (KEEP/KEEP+ANNOTATE/REPLACE-WITH-GRIT/REPLACE-WITH-ICM/REPLACE-WITH-HELPER/ARCHIVE/DELETE/FLAG-FOR-USER). | ADR-0053, ADR-0054, ADR-0118 | [`ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md`](decisions/ADR-0052-inventory-grit-cutover.md) |
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0053 | 2026-05-12 | Accepted | grit + icm + oya-tooling-agent-read as sole sanctioned primitives for agentic work | Fix the agent-callable coordination/state-transition primitive set at {grit, icm, oya-tooling-agent-read}. Direct git/gh permitted only with documented rationale per Directive 12. | None declared | [`ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md`](decisions/ADR-0053-grit-icm-as-sanctioned-primitives.md) |
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0054 | 2026-05-12 | deprecated | Resolve new-crate chicken-and-egg via grit scaffold-claim pattern (icm-coordination-lock fallback) | The canonical scaffold-claim path for new-crate creation is the **icm-coordination-lock fallback**: | ADR-0052, ADR-0053, ADR-0015, ADR-0041 | [`ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md`](decisions/ADR-0054-grit-scaffold-claim-pattern.md) |
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0103 | 2026-05-14 | Accepted | Grit cutover inventory of legacy primitives | Adopt this ADR as the canonical inventory of legacy primitives addressed by the grit cutover, with sanctioned replacements and (where applicable) retirement timing. | None declared | [`ADR-0103-grit-cutover-inventory.md`](decisions/ADR-0103-grit-cutover-inventory.md) |
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0116 | 2026-05-16 | accepted | Retire external agent-coordination tooling (grit, rtk, icm, vox) in favour of the Foundry pipeline | The following external agent-coordination tools are **retired** from the prescribed agent surface in this repo, effective 2026-05-16: | ADR-0054, ADR-0052, ADR-0110, ADR-0115 | [`ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md`](decisions/ADR-0116-retire-external-agent-coordination-tooling.md) |
docs/ADR-INDEX.md:| ADR-0118 | 2026-05-16 | Accepted | Retire archive-orphan fitness lane | Retire the one-time archive-orphan fitness lane and pre-grit archive payload after ADR-0116 establishes the Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) as the canonical VCS substrate. | ADR-0116, ADR-0110, ADR-0111, ADR-0112, ADR-0113, ADR-0052 | [`ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md`](decisions/ADR-0118-retire-archive-orphan-fitness-lane.md) |

docs/standards/claude-code-harness.md:## 3. grit claim→work→done lifecycle
docs/standards/claude-code-harness.md:grit claim <symbol>      # acquire a work-stealing lease on a file::Identifier
docs/standards/claude-code-harness.md:icm recall "<query>"     # bring relevant memory into context
docs/standards/claude-code-harness.md:icm store …              # capture decisions per CLAUDE.md §store triggers
docs/standards/claude-code-harness.md:grit done <symbol>       # release lease + emit audit-chain EVT-GRIT-DONE

specs/microservices/ontology.json:        "purpose": "Git/grit/pre-commit/post-commit hook",
specs/microservices/ontology.json:        "examples": ["hook:pre-commit-validate-artifact", "hook:grit-claim-state-on-stop"],
specs/microservices/ontology.json:      {"id": "I11-grit-claim-coverage", "rule": "Every state-transition event (consumes/heals/generates) MUST be sealed by a grit_done_seal_event_id OR explicitly logged as protocol-violation in audit.", "severity": "warn", "remediation_hint": "Use grit claim/work/done; commit 5880ce0 is a known historical violation per HG-GRIT row."},
specs/root-hub-pointers.json:    "step_2_context_recall": "Prefer Oya VCS / intelligence-native context once available (replaces prior Foundry framing per ADR-0335 Wave 15I); legacy icm recall-context is compatibility/provenance only and is never promotion authority.",
specs/root-hub-pointers.json:    "step_3_oya_vcs_state_transition": "Every agent→repo state transition uses Oya VCS claim → work → verify → done → promote. Legacy grit/icm/rtk/vox/omx/omc surfaces are read/provenance/compatibility only during cutover.",
specs/agent-durable-goal.json:      "rule": "External agent-coordination tooling (grit, rtk, icm, vox) is RETIRED per ADR-0116 (2026-05-16). Use plain git, gh, cargo, jq, sed only. Foundry pipeline (M01-P18) is the canonical agentic workflow substrate.",
specs/agent-durable-goal.json:      "supersedes_directives": ["AGENTIC PIPELINE HARDENING RULE 2026-05-12 (grit-only)", "grit-claim-state-on-stop hook"],
specs/artifact-profile-defaults.json:          "update_mechanism": "post-grit-done-hook",

specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:  "description": "Early masterplan prerequisite for an Oyatie-owned VCS replacement that preserves grit-style agentic-development advantages and upgrades closeout from local merges to GitOps CI/CD promotion.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:    "source_inspiration": "https://github.com/rtk-ai/grit",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "move our vcs replacement for grit earlier",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "apply the same logic / feature as grit but upgrade it",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "look at what makes grit so good for agentic development in rust that avoids merge conflicts and avoids wasted work and integrated into our ci/cd pipeline",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "Oya VCS is our version of grit: an agent-native VCS/CI/CD coordination layer, not just local symbol locks.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "grit, icm, rtk, vox, omx, and omc are being phased out; Oya VCS is set to replace them and must work seamlessly with the Foundry agentic pipeline."
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:    "ordering_rule": "Runs before M01-P08 refresh and before broad M01/M02/M03 fan-out. P00 core is complete; Oya VCS admission is the forward closure authority, while grit/icm-era surfaces remain compatibility/provenance only.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "compatibility_note": "ADR-0116 retires grit/icm/rtk/vox as agent transition authorities; any legacy aliases are import/migration compatibility only and cannot be closure authority."
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "id": "IP-005-grit-compat-cli-and-migration-ratchet",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "deliverable": "Grit-compatible CLI aliases, migration from local grit, CI ratchet banning local-only closeout for agent fan-out."
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "id": "local-grit-only",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "source_path": "https://github.com/rtk-ai/grit",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "summary": "External reference inspected for grit features: AST symbol locks, isolated worktrees, queues, watch, distributed lock stores, and done merge semantics.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:    "scope_note": "Rust remains first-class because grit is strong for Rust AST/symbol claims, but Oyatie is polyglot. The VCS replacement must index every language/runtime surface that can be edited by agents.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:    "core_thesis": "Oya VCS preserves grit-style AST/symbol conflict avoidance, then adds provider-neutral ChangeSets, virtual merge stacks, queue-aware locks, review/CI fix loops, and dev→staging→production promotion.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "Oya VCS is the forward state-transition and promotion authority; legacy grit/icm/rtk/vox/omx/omc are compatibility/provenance only.",
specs/gitops-vcs-replacement.json:      "authority metadata regresses to legacy grit/icm closure authority",
specs/stop-conditions.json:      "condition": "grit status or scaffold-lock ledger shows no unresolved claims owned by this run",
specs/codeview-read-surface.json:      "reason": "Direct git is forbidden by master-plan sequencing; use grit plus codeview/read helpers."
specs/codeview-read-surface.json:      "reason": "Rebase/merge/done/promote state must be owned by Oya VCS GitOps promotion; grit done is legacy compatibility/provenance only."
specs/oyatie-doctrine.json:    "promotion": "oya vcs done + oya vcs promote after seam lane sub-checks, provider evidence, multispectrum evidence, and audit-chain coverage pass; grit done is legacy compatibility/provenance only."
specs/multispectrum-review.json:    "primary_consumer": "agentic_flow (autonomous masterplan execution per ADR-0054 grit + ADR-0069 active-artifact-contract). Human dev_flow is secondary. Agents are the canonical reader of this spec.",
specs/multispectrum-review.json:      "foundry agentic-flow (oya vcs claim \u2192 work \u2192 verify \u2192 done \u2192 promote) with rtk-ai/grit compatibility/provenance only",
specs/multispectrum-review.json:      "description": "Multi-agent autonomous masterplan execution per Oya VCS state machine; ADR-0054/grit-era rows are compatibility/provenance only during cutover.",

specs/active-machine-readable-artifact-contract.json:          "description": "Provenance: which Oya VCS ChangeBundle/Promotion sealed this artifact's last write. Legacy grit done ids are compatibility-only historical inputs."
specs/evidence-taxonomy.json:      "proves": "grit/scaffold lock and ICM lifecycle closed cleanly"
specs/master-plan-sequencing.json:      "fallback": "legacy grit/scaffold locks may record compatibility blockers only when the Oya VCS command surface is unavailable; they are not closure authority"
specs/master-plan-sequencing.json:      "compatibility_window": "P00 core is complete and Oya VCS admission is required for forward agentic fan-out; legacy grit/icm may only preserve historical provenance or unblock a documented command-surface gap."
specs/masterplan.json:      "https://github.com/rtk-ai/grit",
specs/masterplan.json:    "gitops_vcs_directive_2026_05_14": "Move the Oya VCS replacement earlier and upgrade it for polyglot AST/semantic claims, enforced unit/integration/e2e standards, CI/CD review-fix loops, controller-owned rebase/merge queue, and GitOps promotion. ADR-0116 retires grit/icm/rtk/vox as transition authorities.",
specs/masterplan.json:    "oya_vcs_directive_2026_05_14": "Oya VCS is the provider-neutral, agent-native successor to grit with first-class but replaceable GitHub/GitHub Actions/Trivy/Argo CD adapters, native queue feature parity, semantic ChangeSets, virtual merge stack, queue-aware locks, review/CI fix loops, dev/staging/prod promotion, and Rust compile-latency scheduling.",
specs/masterplan.json:    "oya_vcs_directive_2026_05_15": "Oya VCS replaces grit/icm/rtk/vox/omx/omc as forward agentic VCS/promotion authority; legacy surfaces are compatibility/provenance only.",
specs/masterplan.json:              "id": "IP-005-grit-compat-cli-and-migration-ratchet",
specs/masterplan.json:              "deliverable": "Grit-compatible CLI aliases, migration from local grit, CI ratchet banning local-only closeout for agent fan-out.",
specs/masterplan.json:              "path": ".omc/plans/milestones/M01-foundation/phases/P00-gitops-vcs-replacement/IP-005-grit-compat-cli-and-migration-ratchet.md"
specs/masterplan.json:    "summary": "Oya VCS preserves grit-style AST/semantic locks and isolated work, then adds provider-neutral ChangeSets, native/provider-backed semantic merge queues, virtual merge stacks, queue-aware locks, review/CI/security/GitOps fix loops, dev\u2192staging\u2192production release trains, and Rust compile-latency scheduling. GitHub/GitHub Actions, Trivy, and Argo CD are first-class adapters, not core dependencies; native/in-house adapters must provide the same features. It also handles variable-speed agent bursts and many PR views/hour by scheduling ChangeSets rather than provider PRs, applying PR apertures/backpressure/batching, and linking every ChangeSet to provider-neutral OyaWorkItems through a GitHub Issues adapter or native issue tracker.",
specs/masterplan.json:        "id": "IP-005-grit-compat-cli-and-migration-ratchet",
specs/masterplan.json:        "deliverable": "Grit-compatible CLI aliases, migration from local grit, CI ratchet banning local-only closeout for agent fan-out.",
specs/masterplan.json:        "path": ".omc/plans/milestones/M01-foundation/phases/P00-gitops-vcs-replacement/IP-005-grit-compat-cli-and-migration-ratchet.md"

specs/masterplan.json:      "legacy_surfaces": "grit/icm/rtk/vox/omx/omc are compatibility/provenance only and cannot satisfy forward promotion authority",
